2026年 iCatCare 关于猫慢性肾病（CKD）的诊断和管理共识指南 Samantha Taylor 1, Natalie Finch 2, Sarah Caney 3, Jonathan Elliott 4, Rebecca Geddes 5, Femke Mortier 6, Valerie Parker 7, Jessica Quimby 8, Gilad Segev 9, Joanna White 10
2026 iCatCare consensus guidelines on the diagnosis and management of chronic kidney disease in cats Samantha Taylor 1, Natalie Finch 2, Sarah Caney 3, Jonathan Elliott 4, Rebecca Geddes 5, Femke Mortier 6, Valerie Parker 7, Jessica Quimby 8, Gilad Segev 9, Joanna White 10
实际意义：
Practical relevance:
慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）在老年猫中很常见。早期诊断可以促进干预，减缓疾病进展并最小化临床症状。根据国际肾脏研究协会（IRIS）CKD分期系统进行管理，可以延长患病猫的生存期，并保持良好的生活质量。
Chronic kidney disease (CKD) is common in senior cats. Early diagnosis can facilitate interventions to slow progression and minimise clinical signs. Management according to the International Renal Interest Society (IRIS) staging system for CKD can lead to prolonged survival and an excellent quality of life for affected cats.

目标：

Aim:
《2026年iCatCare猫慢性肾病（CKD）诊断与管理共识指南》提供了有关CKD发病机制的信息，关于疾病诊断和分期的指导，以及关于药物和营养治疗的实用内容。指南还讨论了如何优化家庭和兽医诊所环境，以积极影响心理健康，从而促进身体健康，并与整个兽医团队及护理人员合作，减缓疾病进展并最大化生活质量。
The ‘2026 iCatCare consensus guidelines on the diagnosis and management of chronic kidney disease in cats’ provide information on the pathogenesis of CKD, guidance on making a diagnosis and staging of disease, and practical content on medical and nutritional treatment. They also discuss optimising the home and veterinary clinic environment to positively influence mental wellbeing and, in turn, physical health, as well as working with the whole veterinary team and caregivers to slow disease progression and maximise quality of life.
临床挑战：
Clinical challenges:
受影响的猫在慢性肾病（CKD）的早期阶段可能表现出轻微或没有临床症状，这可能导致诊断延迟。该疾病通常是进行性的，管理需要考虑多个因素以处理临床症状。可能出现的并发症如高钙血症、高血压、蛋白尿和贫血，需要进行治疗。此外，由于年龄增长是CKD的风险因素，猫可能存在需要平衡的重要合并症，并对肾脏产生影响。照顾患有CKD的猫可能会增加照护者的负担。因此，支持照护者，根据个体猫的情况进行监测，并让兽医和护理团队共同参与非常重要。
Affected cats may show minimal or no clinical signs in the early stages of CKD, which can result in a delay in diagnosis. The condition is generally progressive and management requires consideration of multiple factors in order to address clinical signs. Complications such as hypercalcaemia, hypertension, proteinuria and anaemia may develop, requiring treatment. Moreover, as advancing age is a risk factor for CKD, cats may have important comorbidities that need to be balanced, with impacts on the kidney. Caring for a cat with CKD can result in caregiver burden. Hence, supporting caregivers, contextualising monitoring to the individual cat, and involving both the veterinary and nursing teams is important.
证据基础：这些指南由国际猫护理（iCatCare）兽医协会召集的专家小组制定。信息基于现有文献、专家意见以及小组成员的经验。
Evidence base: These Guidelines have been created by a panel of experts brought together by International Cat Care (iCatCare) Veterinary Society. Information is based on the available literature, expert opinion and the panel members’ experience.

关键词：肾脏，肌酐，尿液分析，磷，饮食

Keywords: Renal, creatinine, urinalysis, phosphate, diet
慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）是猫常见的疾病原因之一。之前的“ISFM猫慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）的诊断与管理共识指南”由当时的国际猫科医学会（International Society of Feline Medicine）于2016年发布，旨在为临床医生提供有关该疾病的实用信息。然而，在过去的十年中，大量研究已经在病因、早期诊断和管理方面推进了对CKD的理解。《2026年iCatCare猫慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）诊断与管理共识指南》来自国际猫关怀（iCatCare）兽医协会，旨在提供更新的信息——新增了关于合并症、麻醉和镇痛的专门章节——并讨论早期诊断的重要性，以便能够采取措施减缓CKD的进展。此外，伴随这些指南，iCatCare兽医协会还提供了资源——供照护者管理患有CKD的猫，以及供护士和技术员使用，以实现团队协作应对这一重要疾病。
Chronic kidney disease (CKD) is a common cause of illness in cats. The previous ‘ISFM consensus guidelines on the diagnosis and management of feline chronic kidney disease’ 1 were published by the then International Society of Feline Medicine in 2016 to provide clinicians with practical information on the condition. However, over the past 10 years, a large body of research has progressed the understanding of CKD in terms of causes, early diagnosis and management The ‘2026 iCatCare consensus guidelines on the diagnosis and management of chronic kidney disease in cats’ from International Cat Care (iCatCare) Veterinary Society seek both to supply updated information – with the addition of dedicated sections on comorbidities, anaesthesia and analgesia – and to discuss the importance of early diagnosis in order to allow the integration of measures to slow the progression of CKD. In addition, accompanying these Guidelines are resources from iCatCare Veterinary Society – for caregivers on managing a cat with CKD and for nurses and technicians to enable a team-based approach to this important condition.
慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）的流行情况及常规健康检查的重要性 CKD在兽医临床中经常遇到，通常在年轻猫中患病率很低，但随年龄增长而变得更常见。先天性肾脏疾病通常在3岁以下的猫中被发现，此后CKD的患病率下降，并从6岁开始再次上升。CKD是老年猫中最常被诊断的疾病之一，各项研究报告显示12岁以上猫的患病率为28%–81%。在对英国5岁及以上猫的一项调查中，它是最常见的死亡原因；研究中由于肾脏疾病导致的死亡占13.6%，死亡的中位年龄为15岁。在一项瑞典研究中，肾脏疾病也是13岁以下已投保猫的最常见死亡原因。许多CKD猫在疾病晚期才被诊断出来，因为它们在早期阶段几乎没有临床症状。
Prevalence of CKD and the importance of routine health screening CKD is frequently encountered by veterinarians and, generally, the disease has a very low prevalence in young cats, becoming more common with age.2–4 Congenital renal disease is usually recognised in cats <3 years of age, after which the prevalence of CKD decreases and then increases again from 6 years onwards. 5 CKD is one of the most commonly diagnosed diseases in older cats, with studies variously reporting a prevalence of 28–81% in all cats aged >12 years.5–8 It was the most common cause of death in an investigation of cats in the UK aged 5 years or older; death due to renal disease occurred in 13.6% of the study cats, with a median age at death of 15 years. 9 Kidney disease was also the most common cause of death in insured cats dying before the age of 13 years in a Swedish study. 10 Many cats with CKD may be diagnosed at an advanced stage of disease, as they exhibit few clinical signs during the early stages.
然而，早期诊断和干预可以进行监测，并在适当情况下开始饮食和其他治疗措施。因此，建议对年龄⩾7岁的猫进行筛查。筛查通过定期测量体重、血液检查和尿液分析来实现，即使猫没有症状。对于7–10岁的成熟成年猫，建议每年检查一次，而对于老年猫（>10岁），最好每年进行两次常规健康检查。此类健康筛查在比利时根特大学的两项前瞻性研究中发现，在外表健康的老年猫中，有8%的猫被发现患有亚临床慢性肾病（CKD）（国际肾脏兴趣协会 [IRIS] 2期或以上）。在外表健康的老年猫中，疾病发生的频率明显高于成熟成年猫（15% vs 3%）。在首次筛查时被确认健康的猫中，年龄⩾11岁的猫在两年内有24%发展为氮质血症，而7–10岁的猫有8%出现氮质血症。
However, early diagnosis and intervention can allow monitoring, as well as the initiation of dietary and other therapeutic measures, when appropriate. Therefore, screening of cats ⩾7 years of age is recommended. Screening is achieved by performing regular body weight measurement, blood examination and urinalysis, even in asymptomatic cats.1,2 For mature adult cats, aged 7–10 years, annual examinations are recommended, whereas senior cats (>10 years) should preferably be presented twice a year for a routine health check.2,11,12 Such health screening revealed subclinical CKD (International Renal Interest Society [IRIS] stage 2 or higher) in 8% of apparently healthy older cats in two prospective studies at Ghent University, Belgium.13,14 The disease occurred significantly more frequently in healthy-appearing senior cats (15%) compared with mature adult cats (3%). 14 Of cats who were confirmed healthy during the first screening, a further 24% aged ⩾11 years and 8% aged 7–10 years developed azotaemia within 2 years.
14 因此，即使初步健康筛查结果无异常，且猫咪没有氮质血症，也建议对年长猫进行纵向监测，并评估单只猫的连续实验室数据，以便更早发现变化（例如肌酐上升趋势或肌酐的突然增加）。11,12,14,15
14 Therefore, even when initial health screening results are unremarkable, and cats are non-azotaemic, it is advised to monitor older cats longitudinally and evaluate serial laboratory data for the individual cat so that changes (eg, creatinine trending upwards or sudden increases in creatinine) can be identified sooner.11,12,14,15

慢性肾病（CKD）的病理生理学 慢性肾病（CKD）的风险因素

Pathophysiology of CKD Risk factors for CKD
年龄是猫慢性肾病（CKD）最一致确认的风险因素。6 老年猫会出现与肾衰老一致的组织病理学变化，16 表明年龄相关的结构改变可能增加肾脏对进一步损伤的易感性。此外，人们已经认识到猫，包括家猫和野生种类，在CKD方面存在物种相关的易感性，其食肉动物特性被认为可能增加这种风险。17 肾小管脂质积累是一种物种特异性现象，可能在CKD的发病机制和进展中起重要作用。5,16,18 与食肉动物身份相关的饮食因素——如高蛋白摄入19,20、高磷摄入21、增加的饮食酸负荷22以及肠源性尿毒素谱的改变23——可能是CKD发病机制中的潜在风险因素。此外，多种共病状况与CKD的发生或加重风险增加相关。
Age is the most consistently identified risk factor for CKD in cats. 6 Histopathological changes consistent with renal ageing develop in older cats, 16 suggesting that age-associated structural alterations may increase renal susceptibility to further injury. Also, a species-related predisposition to CKD is recognised in felids, including domestic and wild species, and their obligate carnivore status has been suggested to contribute to this risk. 17 Tubular lipid accumulation is a speciesspecific phenomenon that may play an important role in CKD pathogenesis and progression.5,16,18 Dietary factors associated with obligate carnivore status – such as high pro-tein19,20 and phosphate 21 intake, increased dietary acid load 22 and alterations in gut-derived uraemic toxin profiles 23 – may be contributing risk factors in the pathogenesis of Several comorbid conditions are associated with an increased risk of developing or exacerbating CKD.
这些包括甲状腺功能亢进、持续性（包括边缘性）蛋白尿（可能由于高血压引起）、反复的肾缺血事件以及反复的输尿管梗阻。此外，队列研究已将牙周病确定为潜在的风险因素，这表明慢性炎症和系统性疾病可能影响肾脏健康。频繁的疫苗接种状态也被提出作为一个风险因素，尽管目前证据仍有限，需要进一步研究。图1. 小管间质性肾炎的组织学切片（三色染色），这是患有慢性肾病（CKD）猫咪中典型的变化。该图显示在健康小管中夹杂的间质炎症、纤维化和小管丧失的局灶区域（蓝色、紫色）。
These include hyperthyroidism, 24 persistent (including borderline) proteinuria (which may be due to hypertension),25–27 repeated renal ischaemic events and repeated ureteral obstructions.5,28 Additionally, cohort studies have identified periodontal disease as being a potential risk factor,29,30 indicating that chronic inflammatory and systemic conditions may influence renal health. Frequent vaccination status has also been proposed as a risk factor, 29 although current evidence remains limited and further investigation is required. Figure 1. Histology section (trichrome stain) of tubulointerstitial nephritis, the typical change found in cats with chronic kidney disease (CKD). This image illustrates focal areas of interstitial inflammation, fibrosis and tubular loss (blue, purple) interspersed among healthy tubules.
图片由Shannon McLeland提供 猫慢性肾病（CKD）已知病因
Image courtesy of Shannon McLeland Known aetiologies of feline CKD
在大多数患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫中，其疾病的潜在原因无法确定；如果进行肾活检，组织病理学（参见框“慢性肾病（CKD）猫的组织病理学病变”）显示病因不明的慢性小管间质性肾炎。然而，有若干公认的疾病或情况被已知或怀疑为CKD的诱发原因（见框1），如下文讨论所述。
In the majority of cats with CKD, the underlying cause of their disease cannot be determined; if renal biopsies are performed, histopathology (see box, ‘Histopathological lesions in cats with CKD’) reveals chronic tubulointerstitial nephritis of unknown aetiology. 34 However, there are a number of well recognised diseases or conditions that are known or suspected to be an inciting cause of CKD (Box 1), as outlined in the following discussion.

慢性肾脏病的遗传/基因原因

Inherited/genetic causes of CKD
常染色体显性多囊肾病是由PKD1基因突变引起的，是最突出的猫遗传疾病，影响全球38%的波斯猫（占所有猫的6%）。39在阿比西尼亚猫、索马里猫、暹罗猫和东方猫中，可以发现遗传性淀粉样A型淀粉样变性性（AA-淀粉样变性性），其中淀粉样蛋白的沉积可能导致多个器官功能障碍，造成肾功能下降或肝脏破裂及致命性出血。研究仍在进行，以确定哪些基因变异与这种情况相关。40,41此外，已在收容所猫中报告了伴随快速进展慢性肾病（CKD）的AA-淀粉样变性性，假设慢性炎症可能触发了淀粉样蛋白沉积，或可能发生水平传播。42未来，我们对猫特发性慢性肾病（CKD）遗传风险因素的理解可能会进一步扩大。
Autosomal dominant polycystic kidney disease, the result of a mutation in the PKD1 gene, is the most prominent inherited disease of cats, affecting 38% of Persians worldwide (6% of all cats). 39 Hereditary forms of amyloid A amyloidosis (AA-amyloidosis) are recognised in Abyssinian, Somali, Siamese and Oriental cats, in which the deposition of amyloid can lead to dysfunction in multiple organs, resulting in decreasing kidney function or hepatic rupture and fatal haemorrhage. Research is ongoing to characterise which genetic variants are implicated in this condi-tion.40,41 Additionally, AA-amyloidosis has been reported in shelter cats with rapidly progressive CKD, with the hypothesis that chronic inflammation may have triggered amyloid deposition or horizontal transmission may have occurred. 42 In the future, our understanding of the genetic risk factors for idiopathic CKD in cats is likely to expand.
最近的研究发现，在编码猫巨噬细胞凋亡抑制蛋白（一种参与肾脏修复的蛋白）的基因中的一种遗传变异与慢性肾病（CKD）肾功能下降之间存在关联。长期以来，关于肾结石是导致CKD的原因，还是CKD导致泌尿系结石一直存在争议。绝大多数猫的肾结石为草酸钙结石，使其无法溶解。流行病学研究显示，偶发性肾结石与老年猫CKD的进展无关，但移动并导致输尿管梗阻的肾结石可能会导致严重的肾损伤——损伤程度与梗阻的程度和持续时间成正比。
Recent work has identified an association between a genetic variant in the gene encoding for feline apoptosis inhibitor of macrophages (a protein involved in kidney repair) and declining kidney function in CKD. 43 It has long been debated whether nephrolithiasis causes CKD, or if CKD results in urolithiasis. The vast majority of nephroliths in cats are calcium oxalate,44,45 making dissolution impossible. Epidemiological research shows that incidental nephroliths are not associated with progression of CKD in older cats, 46 but nephroliths that move and lead to ureteral obstruction can result in severe kidney damage – the extent of injury being proportional to both the degree and duration of the obstruction.

Figure 2. Ultrasound image of a polycystic kidney in a Persian cat. Image courtesy of Lumbry Park

兽医专家

Veterinary Specialists
图3. 一只11岁猫的肾癌，表现为氮质血症和单侧肾肿大。图片由Samantha Taylor提供。在临床中，单侧输尿管梗阻很容易被忽视，因为当对侧肾功能正常时，氮质血症不会出现。然而，随后的损伤可能导致受累肾明显纤维化和缩小。当肾脏之间的大小差异超过0.7厘米时，被称为“大小肾综合征”（图4），这在33%的患有上尿路（肾和/或输尿管）结石的猫中有记录。图4. “大小肾综合征”发生在此前未被发现的单侧输尿管梗阻导致一侧肾受损，从而随着时间的推移导致该肾纤维化和缩小。对侧肾肥大以帮助提高整体肾小球滤过率（GFR）。如果第二只肾也发生输尿管梗阻，大小差异可能会加剧，这可能是猫出现氮质血症和不适症状的时间点。
Figure 3. Renal carcinoma in an 11-year-old cat presenting with azotaemia and unilateral renomegaly. Image courtesy of Samantha Taylor Unilateral ureteral obstruction can easily be overlooked in the clinic because azotaemia will not develop when the contralateral kidney has normal function. However, subsequent damage can lead to marked fibrosis and shrinking of the affected kidney. A disparity in size of >0.7 cm between the kidneys is referred to as ‘big kidney-little kidney’ syndrome (Figure 4), 47 and this has been documented in 33% of cats with upper urinary tract (kidney and/or ureter) stones. 45 Figure 4. ‘Big kidney-little kidney’ syndrome occurs when a prior, undetected, unilateral ureteral obstruction results in damage to one kidney, leading to fibrosis and shrinkage of that kidney over time. The contralateral kidney hypertrophies to help increase the global glomerular filtration rate. The size difference may be exacerbated if the second kidney suffers a ureteral obstruction, and this may be the point at which the cat presents as azotaemic and unwell.

图片由Rebecca Geddes提供

Image courtesy of Rebecca Geddes
当发现输尿管梗阻时，由于手术干预可改善结局，因此建议进行手术治疗而非药物治疗。然而，药物治疗也可以取得良好效果，较年轻的年龄和更远端的小型尿路结石与治疗成功显著相关。手术治疗以恢复肾脏到膀胱的尿液流动（例如，皮下输尿管分流术放置、输尿管再植术）后，应将猫考虑为患有慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）；在未来疾病进展方面，这似乎与特发性CKD类似。
When ureteral obstruction is identified, surgical intervention is recommended over medical management due to improved out-comes. 48 However, medical management can result in good outcomes, with younger age and more distal small uroliths showing a significant association with successful treat-ment. 49 After surgical management to restore urine flow from the kidney to the bladder (eg, subcutaneous ureteral bypass placement, ureteral reimplantation), cats should be considered to have CKD; this appears analogous to idiopathic CKD in terms of future disease progression. 50
急性肾损伤（AKI） 任何由于肾脏损伤引起的AKI原因（例如，肾盂肾炎、毒素或药物相关、缺血性损伤或肾后梗阻）都应被视为随后发展为CKD的一个促成因素。
Acute kidney injury Any cause of AKI due to renal insult (eg, pyelonephritis, toxin or drug-related, ischaemic injury or postrenal obstruction) should be considered to be a contributing factor to the subsequent development of CKD. 51

高钙血症和肾钙沉着

Hypercalcaemia and nephrocalcinosis
肾钙沉积是指钙在肾实质中的沉积，在猫中，通常表现为肾髓质中的磷酸钙沉积。52 与非氮质血症的猫相比，氮质血症慢性肾病（CKD）猫的组织学肾钙沉积发生率更高。53 肾钙沉积与CKD进展之间的关系较为复杂。回顾性数据显示，肾钙沉积的发展需要时间，因为患有快速进展性CKD且生存时间较短的猫在尸检中最不可能显示显微镜下的肾钙沉积。52 针对高钙血症猫咪的前瞻性研究显示，接受肾脏饮食管理的猫更可能出现肾钙沉积。54 反过来，与未发生肾钙沉积的肾脏饮食猫相比，这些猫血清肌酐、磷和FGF23随时间的升高更明显，这表明肾钙沉积可能促进CKD的进展。54 因此，在喂食肾脏饮食时，应监测血清钙（最好是离子钙）。
Nephrocalcinosis is the deposition of calcium in the parenchyma of the kidney, which, in cats, typically consists of calcium phosphate deposits in the renal medulla. 52 The incidence of histological nephrocalcinosis is higher in cats with azotaemic CKD compared with non-azotaemic cats. 53 The relationship between nephrocalcinosis and the progression of CKD is complex. Retrospective data indicate that it takes time for nephrocalcinosis to develop, because cats with rapidly progressive CKD and shorter survival times are the least likely to demonstrate microscopic nephrocalcinosis at postmortem examination. 52 Prospective studies of hypercalcaemic cats managed on renal diets have shown that they are more likely to develop nephrocalcinosis. 54 In turn, this is associated with a greater increase in serum creatinine, phosphate and FGF23 over time when compared with cats fed a renal diet who did not develop nephrocalcinosis, suggesting that nephrocalcinosis may contribute to CKD pro-gression. 54 Thus, when feeding a renal diet, serum calcium (preferably ionised) should be monitored.
如果发生高钙血症，则需要采取措施降低血清钙水平（见“高钙血症的管理”）。
If hypercalcaemia develops, measures to reduce serum calcium will be needed (see ‘Management of hypercalcaemia’).

肾小球疾病

Glomerular diseases
肾小球肾病是慢性肾病（CKD）的少见原因，但识别它们非常重要。原发性肾小球疾病包括免疫复合物肾小球肾炎（ICGN）和非免疫复合物肾小球肾炎。ICGN 是蛋白尿性肾脏疾病的重要原因，通常影响年轻猫，它们表现为氮质血症、明显蛋白尿、低白蛋白血症、贫血、肾肿大，在某些情况下还可能出现浆细胞足皮炎。由于早期诊断和管理可能增加疾病控制的机会，因此在氮质血症猫中应进行尿液分析，包括蛋白定量。
Glomerulonephropathies are an uncommon cause of CKD but important to recognise. Primary glomerular diseases include ICGN and non-immune-complex glomerulonephri-tis. 55 ICGN is a significant cause of proteinuric renal disease, typically affecting younger cats who present with azotaemia, marked proteinuria, hypoalbuminaemia, anaemia, reno-megaly 33 and, in some cases, plasma cell pododermatitis. 56 As early diagnosis and management may increase the chances of disease control, urinalysis, including quantification of protein, should be performed in azotaemic

原发性醛固酮增多症

Primary hyperaldosteronism
原发性醛固酮增多症是由于肾上腺肿瘤或增生引起的醛固酮分泌过多所致，这在猫中是一种罕见的内分泌疾病，但慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）是常见的合并症。57 醛固酮过多可能通过刺激纤维化直接促使CKD的发展，并且由于伴随的系统性高血压还可能导致进一步的肾脏损伤。58 重要的是，患有CKD的猫可能存在肾上腺增生，因此在诊断醛固酮增多症之前，需要进行适当的诊断测试。59
Primary hyperaldosteronism, which is the result of excessive aldosterone secretion secondary to adrenal neoplasia or hyperplasia, is an uncommon endocrinopathy in cats, but CKD is a frequently reported comorbidity. 57 It is possible that excess aldosterone contributes directly to the development of CKD through stimulation of fibrosis and that further renal damage occurs due to associated systemic hypertension. 58 Importantly, cats with CKD may have adrenal gland hyperplasia, so appropriate diagnostic testing is indicated before making a diagnosis of hyperaldosteronism. 59

CKD的进展

Progression of CKD
慢性肾病（CKD）的进展可以遵循两条主要路径。第一条路径中，由于持续的肾内过程，疾病逐渐且持续发展，最终导致肾单位的丧失，并将功能性实质替换为纤维化组织。第二条路径则呈阶梯性发展特征，以离散的急性肾损伤（AKI）发作为标志。其中一些发作可能是亚临床的，尚未被发现，但仍会促进疾病进展，而其他发作则在临床上明显，并被归类为急性加重型慢性肾病（CKD）（ACKD）。这些路径的相对流行率及其影响仍未完全了解。所有已知的AKI病因，包括缺血、感染、炎症和肾毒性，都可能触发ACKD。在一项关于猫ACKD的研究中，缺血、尿路梗阻和肾盂肾炎是最常见的推测原因；然而，在66%的病例中，病因在就诊和整个疾病过程中仍未确定。AKI和CKD传统上被视为不同的临床实体。
CKD progression can follow two principal pathways. In the first, disease advances gradually and continuously due to ongoing intrarenal processes, ultimately leading to loss of nephrons and replacement of the functional parenchyma with fibrotic tissue. The second involves a stepwise trajectory characterised by discrete episodes of AKI. Some of these episodes may be subclinical and go unnoticed, yet still contribute to disease progression, while others are clinically evident and classified as acute-on-chronic kidney disease (ACKD).60,61 The relative prevalence and the impact of these pathways remain incompletely understood. All the known aetiologies of AKI, including ischaemia, infection, inflammation and nephrotoxicity, are potential triggers of ACKD. In a study of ACKD in cats, ischaemia, ureteral obstruction and pyelonephritis were the most common presumed causes; yet, in 66% of cases, the aetiology remained unidentified at presentation and throughout the course of disease. 61 AKI and CKD have traditionally been regarded as distinct clinical entities.
然而，越来越多的证据表明存在双向相互作用——慢性肾病（CKD）是急性肾损伤（AKI）的一个危险因素，61 而 AKI 发作可能会启动或加速 CKD 的进展。51
However, increasing evidence suggests a bidirectional interplay – with CKD representing a risk factor for AKI, 61 and AKI episodes potentially initiating or accelerating the progression of CKD. 51

与慢性肾病（CKD）进展相关的因素

Factors associated with the progression of CKD
多项研究的结果显示，猫慢性肾病（CKD）进展与多个因素相关。蛋白尿的严重程度与生存时间密切相关，而尿蛋白/肌酐比值（UPC）则是诊断后前12个月CKD进展的独立预测因素。涉及CKD–矿物质和骨疾病（CKD-MBD；见框2）的变量也与CKD进展和预后相关：血清磷浓度升高与诊断后12个月内的病程进展相关，同时也与较短的生存时间相关；在氮质血症性CKD诊断时血清成纤维细胞生长因子（FGF-23）23（FGF23）浓度升高亦与疾病进展和死亡率相关。在诊断后头3个月的监测能够帮助预测进展，表明连续监测肌酐、磷和体重可以识别进展性CKD猫与病情稳定猫；进展性CKD猫的生存时间显著较短（>60%在诊断365天内死亡）。
Several factors are associated with CKD progression in cats, as illustrated by the findings of a number of studies. The severity of proteinuria is strongly associated with survival time, 62 while the UPC is an independent predictor of CKD progression within the first 12 months following diagnosis. 63 Variables involved in CKD–mineral and bone disorder (CKD-MBD; Box 2) are also associated with CKD progression and outcomes: increased serum phosphate concentration is associated with progression within 12 months of diagno-sis 63 and also with a short survival time; 70 higher serum FGF23 concentration at the point of diagnosis of azotaemic CKD is similarly associated with both disease progression and mortality. 71 Monitoring in the first 3 months from diagnosis can aid prediction of progression, suggesting serial monitoring of creatinine, phosphate and body weight can identify cats with progressive CKD vs stable disease; cats with progressive CKD have a significantly shorter survival time (>60% dying within 365 days of diagnosis).
72 与慢性肾脏病进展相关的其他因素包括贫血、63 尿毒素（见框 3）74 和低钾血症。77
72 Other factors associated with progression of CKD include anaemia, 63 uraemic toxins (Box 3) 74 and hypokalaemia. 77

慢性肾病（CKD）的临床表现

Clinical signs of CKD
与慢性肾病（CKD）相关的临床体征通常起病隐匿且往往非特异性。历史和最近的研究中最常报告的症状是多尿（PU）、多饮（PD）和体重减轻，其次是食欲下降和呕吐。78–80 其他常见症状包括嗜睡和虚弱，尽管许多猫没有临床症状，可能只能通过健康筛查确诊。14,80,81 体重减轻（图6）通常逐渐出现，82 可能由于与CKD并发症相关的恶心和不适引起，如低钾血症、贫血、酸中毒、便秘和脱水，以及由于食欲抑制物质积累导致的食欲调节异常。83 图6。
Clinical signs associated with CKD are typically insidious in onset and often non-specific. The most commonly reported signs, both historically and in more recent studies, have been polyuria (PU), polydipsia (PD) and weight loss, followed by loss of appetite and vomit-ing.78–80 Other common signs include lethargy and weakness, although many cats show no clinical signs and may only be diagnosed via health screening.14,80,81 Weight loss (Figure 6), which is typically gradual in onset, 82 may occur due to nausea and malaise associated with complications of CKD, such as hypokalaemia, anaemia, acidosis, constipation and dehydration, as well as appetite dysregu-lation due to accumulation of anorexigenic substances. 83 Figure 6.
体重减轻在患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫中很常见，可能是唯一的临床症状。这只病猫还表现出毛发状况不佳和肌肉状况下降，这些都是患有CKD的猫的典型表现。图片由Sarah Caney提供。隐匿性的体重减轻和肌肉量丢失可能会被照顾者忽视，或被错误地归因于年龄。一项对500多只猫进行的大型研究报告称，在CKD诊断前12个月中，中位体重下降为8.9%，但发现体重减轻在诊断前3年就已出现，并且在诊断后加速发生。未预期的体重减轻或身体状况评分（BCS）或肌肉状况评分（MCS）的恶化都不应被忽视，应考虑进行进一步的诊断检测。
Weight loss is common in cats with CKD and may be the only clinical sign. This patient also has a poor haircoat and reduced muscle condition, which are further typical findings in cats with CKD. Image courtesy of Sarah Caney Insidious weight loss and loss of muscle mass may go unnoticed by caregivers or be incorrectly attributed to age. One large study of over 500 cats reported a median loss of 8.9% body weight in the 12 months before diagnosis of CKD, but found that weight loss was already present 3 years before diagnosis and accelerated after diagnosis. 82 Unintended weight loss or a deterioration in body condition score (BCS) or muscle condition score (MCS) should not be ignored, and should prompt consideration of further diagnostic testing.
慢性肾病（CKD）较少见的迹象包括由于低钾血症导致的颈部腹屈（图7）84，以及由于尿路感染（UTI）和便秘引起的下尿路症状。85,86 便秘可能表明慢性脱水和/或低钾血症。患有晚期CKD的猫可能出现尿毒症口臭和口腔溃疡（图8）。79 图7. 由于低钾血症相关的猫的颈部腹屈和全身虚弱
Less frequent signs of CKD include neck ventroflexion due to hypokalaemia (Figure 7) 84 and lower urinary tract signs due to urinary tract infection (UTI) and constipation.85,86 Constipation may indicate chronic dehydration and/or hypokalaemia. Uraemic halitosis and oral ulceration may develop in cats with advanced CKD (Figure 8). 79 Figure 7. Neck ventroflexion and generalised weakness in a cat due to hypokalaemia associated with
CKD。图片由Sarah Caney提供 图8。因严重氮质血症，猫可能出现痛苦的尿毒性口腔溃疡和口臭
CKD. Image courtesy of Sarah Caney Figure 8. Painful uraemic oral ulcers and halitosis may develop in cats with severe azotaemia due to
慢性肾病（CKD）。图片由Sarah Caney提供。确诊慢性肾病（CKD）
CKD. Image courtesy of Sarah Caney Making a diagnosis of CKD
虽然尚未建立标准化的猫咪诊断标准，但猫慢性肾病（CKD）通常按照类比于人类CKD的原则来定义，要求记录慢性（例如，≥3个月）的功能障碍或肾脏结构改变。然而，从实际操作的角度来看，对于有临床症状或处于较高阶段CKD的猫来说，推迟干预以确认持续性变化并不推荐。尽早确认持续性氮质血症是可取的，以便加快干预（例如，两周后重复检测）。在临床环境中，诊断可能基于血清肌酐浓度高于实验室参考范围（RI），结合不适当的低尿比重（USG）或持续性氮质血症（当无法获取尿液时）。此外，还会考虑其他支持性发现，如影像学或肾脏触诊显示的结构改变和/或蛋白尿。
Although standardised diagnostic criteria for cats have not been established, feline CKD is generally defined following principles analogous to human CKD, 87 requiring documentation of chronic (eg, ⩾3 months) functional impairment or structural renal change. However, in practical terms, postponing interventions to confirm persistent change would not be recommended in cats with clinical signs or higher stage CKD. Confirmation of persistent azotaemia sooner is desirable in order to expedite interventions (eg, repeat testing 2 weeks later). In the clinical setting, a diagnosis may be based on a serum creatinine concentration above the laboratory reference interval (RI), in combination with an inappropriately low urine specific gravity (USG) or persistent azotaemia (when urine cannot be obtained). In addition, consideration is given to other supportive findings such as evidence of structural change on imaging or kidney palpation and/or proteinuria.
即使其他肾功能指标仍在参考范围内，如果美国尿比重（USG）低于1.035，也应怀疑尿液浓缩能力下降和早期慢性肾病（CKD），尤其是在多次测量中持续出现这种情况时。在这种情况下，包括血清肌酐的连续测量和诊断影像学检查在内的进一步调查可以支持CKD的诊断。并非所有CKD猫都会符合所有标准；例如，可根据诊断影像学的结构性变化或持续性蛋白尿（UPC >0.4）而没有氮质血症来诊断CKD（参见“IRIS慢性肾病（CKD）分期”）。临床医生在诊断CKD时应注意的其他重要方面在框4中有说明。
A USG <1.035 should raise suspicion for decreased urineconcentrating ability and early-stage CKD, even when other markers of kidney function remain within the RI, particularly when documented consistently over time. In such cases, further investigations including serial measurement of serum creatinine and diagnostic imaging can support a diagnosis of CKD. Not all cats with CKD will meet all criteria; for example, CKD can be diagnosed based on structural changes on diagnostic imaging or persistent proteinuria (UPC >0.4) without azotaemia (see ‘IRIS staging of CKD’). Other important aspects that clinicians should be aware of in relation to the diagnosis of CKD are addressed in Box 4.

病史与体格检查

History and physical examination
在前一节“CKD 的临床表现”中讨论了相关的病史发现。CKD 猫的体格检查可能没有明显异常，但提示性的发现包括因贫血引起的苍白（图 9）、脱水的临床证据、因全身性高血压导致的靶器官损伤的迹象（表 5）、肾脏触诊异常（通常为小而硬的肾脏）、结肠中坚硬的粪便，以及体况评分（BCS）和/或肌肉状况评分（MCS）降低。猫还应评估是否存在共病的迹象，如牙周疾病、心脏疾病、甲状腺功能亢进、认知功能障碍综合征和退行性关节疾病（DJD）（见“CKD 与重要共病”）。
Pertinent historical findings were discussed in the earlier section, ‘Clinical signs of CKD’. Physical examination may be unremarkable in cats with CKD, but suggestive findings include pallor due to anaemia (Figure 9), clinical evidence of dehydration, signs of target organ damage due to systemic hypertension (Box 5), 90 abnormal renal palpation (often small, firm kidneys), firm faeces in the colon, and reduced BCS and/or MCS.78,79 Cats should also be assessed for signs of comorbidities such as periodontal disease, cardiac disease, hyperthyroidism, cognitive dysfunction syndrome and degenerative joint disease (DJD) (see ‘CKD and important comorbidities’).
图 9. CKD 导致非再生性贫血的猫的苍白。图片由 Sarah Caney 提供
Figure 9. Pallor in a cat with non-regenerative anaemia due to CKD. Image courtesy of Sarah Caney

血清肌酐

Serum creatinine
肌酐是最广泛使用的肾小球滤过率（GFR）替代标志物。与尿素相比，它是首选标志物，因为它受非肾脏因素的影响较小，因此具有更高的特异性。然而，肌酐浓度也有其局限性：它受肌肉质量和水合状态的影响。此外，参考区间（RI）在不同参考实验室和分析方法之间存在差异。肌酐还显示出与GFR的非线性关系；因此，在血清肌酐超过实验室参考区间之前，GFR可能已经显著下降。理想情况下，应使用针对猫所处人群设计的参考区间。由于慢性肾病（CKD）在老年猫中更常见，对于7岁及以上的猫，最好使用适合老年猫的年龄相关参考区间。
Creatinine is the most widely used surrogate marker of GFR. It is the preferred marker over urea as it is less affected by non-renal factors and so has greater specificity. 91 However, creatinine concentration has its limitations: it is influenced by muscle mass and hydration status. Moreover, RIs vary among reference laboratories and analytical methods. 92 Creatinine also demonstrates a non-linear relationship with GFR; therefore, a substantial decline in GFR might occur prior to an increase in serum creatinine above the laboratory RI. 93 Ideally, an RI designed for the population to which the cat belongs is used. Since CKD occurs more commonly in older cats, age-appropriate RIs for older cats may best be used in cats aged ⩾7 years.
94 有研究表明，老年猫的血清肌酐水平较低，这可能是由于瘦体重的减少，因此肌酐参考区间的上限需要向下调整。95,96 此外，如果之前有血液检查结果，可进行“趋势分析”（见框4），理想情况下应使用同一家实验室进行测量。图10. 血压测量应作为临床检查的第一部分进行，并尽可能在安静的环境下进行，尽量减少操作，使猫能够采取自然的姿势。此处猫处于笼子的底部，袖带围在尾巴上。图片由Sarah Caney提供。图11.
94 It has been shown that serum creatinine is lower in older cats, possibly due to loss of lean muscle mass, and so the upper limit of the RI for creatinine needs to be adjusted downwards.95,96 Additionally, ‘trending’ can be performed if previous blood examination results are available (see Box 4), ideally using the same laboratory for measurements. Figure 10. Blood pressure measurement should be performed as the first part of the clinical examination and in as calm a manner as possible, with minimal handling, allowing the cat to adopt a natural position. Here the cat is in the base of their carrier, with the cuff placed around the tail. Image courtesy of Sarah Caney Figure 11.
远距离间接检眼镜检查。该技术在昏暗的房间内进行（为了这张图片的目的，这里有日光），检查者站在离猫一臂之遥的地方，将光源指向猫的眼睛。应调整光束的角度，直到看到明亮的Tapetum反射；然后将一块20–30屈光度的手持镜片置于眼睛正前方，与光束垂直。镜片的“凸面”（鼓起的一面）应朝向检查者（“凸面对凸面”）。可以获得视网膜的倒立影像，且镜片的放大倍数通常确保一次即可看到大部分视网膜。图片由Sarah Caney提供 图12。
Distant indirect ophthalmoscopy. The technique is performed in a darkened room (for the purposes of this image, there is daylight), with the examiner standing at arm’s length from the cat, directing the light source towards the cat’s eye. The angle of the light beam should be adjusted until a bright tapetal reflection is seen; then a 20–30 dioptre hand lens is placed just in front of the eye, perpendicular to the beam of light. The ‘belly’ (bulging side) of the lens should be facing the examiner (‘belly to belly’). An inverted image of the fundus is obtained and the lens magnification typically ensures that much of the fundus is visible in one view. Image courtesy of Sarah Caney Figure 12.
在高达80%的系统性高血压猫咪中存在眼部靶器官损伤，表现为 (a) 前部变化，如前房出血和/或 (b) 视网膜和脉络膜变化，如水肿、出血和脱离。图片由Sarah Caney提供
Ocular target organ damage is present in up to 80% of cats with systemic hypertension and can manifest as (a) anterior changes such as hyphaema and/or (b) retinal and choroidal changes such as oedema, haemorrhage and detachment. Images courtesy of Sarah Caney

对称性二甲基精氨酸

Symmetric dimethylarginine
SDMA 是一种较新可用的 GFR 生物标志物。最初的研究显示，SDMA 在检测肾功能减退方面比肌酐表现出更高的敏感性，有些研究表明，在患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫中，SDMA 的升高可能在肌酐超过参考区间上限前 17 个月就被记录到。95 然而，随后研究显示 SDMA 与 GFR 的相关性，以及肌酐与 GFR 的相关性是相当的。97,98 与肌酐不同，SDMA 浓度受肌肉量影响较小，并且在炎症状态下及肌肉量丧失的动物中可能更可靠。99,100 肾前性和非肾性因素会影响血清 SDMA 浓度。重要的是，这包括老年猫的甲状腺功能亢进，其中除 GFR 外的因素可能导致血清 SDMA 升高（参见“CKD 及重要共病”）。101,102 淋巴瘤可升高 SDMA 浓度。103 相反，糖尿病猫的 SDMA 浓度通常明显低于健康对照组或患有肾脏或心脏疾病的猫。
SDMA is a more recently available biomarker of GFR. Initial studies showed that SDMA demonstrated greater sensitivity than creatinine for the detection of reduced renal function, with some studies indicating that elevations in SDMA might be documented 17 months before creatinine exceeds the upper limit of the RI in cats with CKD. 95 Subsequent research, however, has shown that the correlation between SDMA and GFR, and creatinine and GFR, is comparable.97,98 Unlike creatinine, SDMA concentrations are less affected by muscle mass and may be more reliable during inflammatory states and in animals with loss of muscle mass.99,100 Pre-renal and non-renal factors influence serum SDMA concentrations. Importantly, this includes hyperthyroidism in older cats, where factors other than GFR may lead to elevations in serum SDMA (see ‘CKD and important comor-bidities’).101,102 Lymphoma can elevate SDMA concentrations. 103 Conversely, cats with diabetes mellitus often have significantly lower SDMA concentrations than healthy controls or those with renal or cardiac disease.
104 对于年长猫咪，已经提出了比标准商业免疫分析阈值（>14 µg/dl）更高的SDMA诊断阈值（⩾18 µg/dl）。
104 For older cats, a higher diagnostic threshold (⩾18 µg/dl) than the standard commercial immunoassay cut-off (>14 µg/dl) has been proposed for SDMA. 96

血清总钙和/或离子钙

Serum total and/or ionised calcium
患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫咪的钙浓度可能偏低、正常或升高，应作为CKD-MBD的一部分进行解读。总钙反映蛋白结合、复合以及离子化的钙分数，但只有离子化钙分数具有生物活性并且与临床相关。因此，建议在可能的情况下直接测量患CKD猫咪的离子化钙（iCa）。在被诊断时，约有20%的CKD猫咪存在离子化高钙血症，通常较轻微，但可能通过进一步降低肾小球滤过率（GFR）并导致食欲减退、多尿/多饮（PU/PD）、肌肉无力、便秘、尿结石及软组织钙化而加重CKD。伴随高钙血症的氮质血症的识别可能难以解读，因为CKD可能促进高钙血症，而离子化高钙血症又可降低GFR；因此，根据具体病例表现，临床医生应考虑评估其他原因，如肿瘤或饮食相关因素。
Calcium concentrations in cats with CKD may be low, normal or increased, and should be interpreted as part of CKD-MBD. Total calcium reflects protein-bound, complexed and ionised fractions, but only the ionised fraction is biologically active and clinically relevant. Therefore, direct measurement of ionised calcium (iCa) is recommended whenever possible in cats with CKD. Ionised hypercalcaemia is present at diagnosis in 20% of cats with CKD, 110 and is typically mild, yet can exacerbate CKD by further lowering GFR and contributing to anorexia, PU/PD, muscle weakness, constipation, urolithiasis and soft tissue calcification. Identification of azotaemia concurrent with hypercalcaemia can be challenging to interpret because CKD may promote hypercal-caemia, while ionised hypercalcaemia can reduce GFR; therefore, dependent on case presentation, clinicians should consider evaluating for additional causes such as neoplasia or dietrelated factors.

血清磷

Serum phosphate
肾脏在磷酸盐调节中起关键作用。由于肾脏排磷减少，慢性肾脏病（CKD）会出现高磷血症，尽管在疾病的早期阶段，由于甲状旁腺激素（PTH）和成纤维细胞生长因子（FGF-23）23（FGF23）的调节机制，磷酸盐浓度通常仍在参考区间内。因此，血清磷不应被视为CKD的敏感标志物。
The kidneys play a key role in phosphate regulation. Hyperphosphataemia develops in CKD because of decreased renal excretion of phosphate, although phosphate concentrations are often within the RI in earlier stages of disease because of PTH and FGF23 regulatory mechanisms.111– 113 Therefore, serum phosphate should not be considered a sensitive marker of CKD.

肾小球滤过率（GFR）

Glomerular filtration rate
GFR 被认为是最准确、最灵敏的肾功能测量指标，尽管参考区间较宽，而且个体猫的测量值可能随时间变化。在确诊为高氮质血症性慢性肾病（CKD）的猫中，通常不需要评估 GFR，因为它不会提供超过血清肌酐浓度升高之外的有用诊断信息。在怀疑早期非高氮质血症性 CKD 或在需要精确评估肾功能以指导安全使用或剂量调整经肾排泄药物的情况下，可以考虑测量 GFR。在这种情况下，可使用简化的单样本艾嗪酰洛尔清除协议来估算猫的 GFR，但在实际操作中很少进行。伴随 CKD 的猫中常见正常细胞型、正常染色、非再生性贫血，尤其是在疾病晚期。因此，应进行全血细胞计数，或者至少评估血细胞压积（PCV）并进行血涂片检查。患有肾盂肾炎的猫可能会表现出一些症状。诊断工作中最重要的尿液参数是尿比重（USG）和尿蛋白。
GFR is considered the most accurate and sensitive measure of kidney function, although RIs are wide and measurements can vary temporally in an individual cat. 91 In cats with established azotaemic CKD, assessment of GFR is rarely indicated because it does not add useful diagnostic information beyond an increase in serum creatinine concentration. Measurement of GFR may be considered in suspected early, non-azotaemic CKD, or when precise evaluation of renal function is required to guide the safe use or dosing of renally excreted drugs. In such cases, simplified and single-sample iohexol clearance protocols are available for estimating GFR in cats, but are rarely performed in practice.91,93,114 Normocytic normochromic non-regenerative anaemia is common in cats with CKD,34,63 especially in the later stages of disease. Thus, a complete blood count, or, as a minimum, assessment of packed cell volume (PCV) and a blood smear review, is indicated. Cats with pyelonephritis may exhibit The most important urinary parameters to evaluate as part of the diagnostic work-up are USG and urine protein.
✜ 尿液浓缩能力 肾功能下降的最早表现之一是尿液浓缩能力下降（尿比重（USG） USG <1.035）。115,116 在许多情况下，尿液浓缩能力下降或丧失往往发生在血清肌酐超过参考范围上限之前。79 然而，尽管尿液浓缩是肾功能下降的最早标志之一，早期慢性肾病（CKD）且肌酐轻度升高的猫可能仍有适当浓缩的尿液（即尿比重（USG） USG ⩾1.035）。除非猫咪饮食中含有高水分，或有其他导致尿液稀释的原因，否则尿比重（USG）低于1.035应提醒临床医生考虑CKD的可能性，并在持续存在时进行重复评估和进一步检查。91 图13.
✜ Urine-concentrating ability One of the earliest manifestations of a decline in kidney function is a reduction in urine-concentrating ability (USG <1.035).115,116 In many cases, urine-concentrating ability decreases or is lost before serum creatinine exceeds the upper limit of the RI. 79 However, despite urine concentration being one of the earliest markers of decreased kidney function, cats with early-stage CKD and mildly elevated creatinine could have appropriately concentrated urine (ie, USG ⩾1.035). Unless a cat is being fed a highmoisture diet, or has another reason for producing dilute urine, a USG <1.035 should alert the clinician to the possibility of CKD and prompt repeated assessment and further investigation, if persistent. 91 Figure 13.
在可能的情况下，应通过膀胱穿刺获取尿液用于细菌培养。该技术可以在最少的操作下进行，让猫保持舒适的站立姿势，并提供食物作为分散注意力的手段。图片由Jessica Quimby提供
Where possible, urine should be obtained via cystocentesis for bacterial culture. The technique can be performed using minimal handling, with the cat in a comfortable standing position and food offered as a distraction. Image courtesy of Jessica Quimby

诊断影像

Diagnostic imaging
影像学诊断是评估出现氮质血症或怀疑肾脏疾病的猫的重要组成部分。其目的是确诊固有肾脏疾病，同时排除肾后性氮质血症（即输尿管梗阻和/或肾积水；图14）或其他导致氮质血症的原因，如肾肿瘤、137多囊肾病或感染性原因（猫传染性腹膜炎、肾盂肾炎）。138 当血清肌酐和其他生物标志物在早期（IRIS 1期）疾病中为正常时，影像学（X线、超声、CT）也可显示慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）的存在。影像学上典型的慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）表现包括肾脏体积小、边缘不规则以及皮质髓质分界丧失（图15）。138 在患有慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）的猫中，还可能观察到的其他表现包括肾脏矿化、肾结石或皮质条纹。
Diagnostic imaging is an important part of the assessment of cats presenting with azotaemia or suspected renal disease. The aim is to rule in intrinsic renal disease, as well as rule out postrenal azotaemia (ie, ureteral obstruction and/or hydronephrosis; Figure 14) or other causes of azotaemia such as renal neoplasia, 137 polycystic kidney disease or infectious causes (feline infectious peritonitis, pyeloneph-ritis). 138 Imaging (radiographs, ultrasound, CT) may also demonstrate the presence of CKD when serum creatinine and other biomarkers are normal in early (IRIS stage 1) disease. Findings typical of CKD on imaging include small kidney size, irregular margins and loss of corticomedullary definition (Figure 15). 138 Additional findings that may be observed in cats with CKD include renal mineralisation, nephroliths or striations in the cortex.
✜ 蛋白尿 在猫慢性肾病（CKD）的诊断和评估中，一个重要且具有临床相关性的参数是蛋白尿。持续高水平的肾性蛋白尿最常反映肾小球损伤，这可能是疾病的诱发原因，并且可能在血清肌酐明显升高之前就存在。55 蛋白尿也可能是超滤和高血压的结果，因此是肾小管间质疾病的继发性后果。63,117 当蛋白尿（UPC >0.2）与其他异常情况如尿素血症、异常的尿液浓缩能力、结构性变化或SDMA升高同时存在时，可支持CKD的诊断。此外，UPC的大小提供预后信息，并与疾病进展相关，因此识别和监测这一尿液参数是猫CKD评估的重要组成部分（参见“IRIS CKD分期”）。62,118 UPC应与尿沉渣检查和生化结果一起解读，以排除肾前和肾后性蛋白尿。
✜ Proteinuria An important and clinically relevant parameter in the diagnosis and evaluation of CKD in cats is proteinuria. A persistent high-magnitude renal proteinuria most commonly reflects glomerular injury, which may be the inciting cause of the disease and may be present before marked increases in serum creatinine occur. 55 Proteinuria may also be the result of hyperfiltration and hypertension, and thus a secondary consequence of tubular interstitial disease.63,117 The presence of proteinuria (UPC >0.2) in conjunction with other abnormalities, such as azotaemia, inappropriate urine-concentrating ability, structural changes or increases in SDMA, supports a diagnosis of CKD. In addition, the magnitude of UPC provides prognostic information and is associated with disease progression, making the identification and monitoring of this urinary parameter an integral component of CKD assessment in cats (see ‘IRIS staging of CKD’).62,118 UPC should be interpreted alongside sediment examination and biochemistry results to exclude preand post-renal proteinuria.
✜ 尿沉渣检查 在疑似慢性肾病（CKD）的猫的尿液分析中，另一个必不可少的部分是尿沉渣检查，它提供了临床相关信息，可用于识别疾病的原因或提示疾病的并发症（例如，结晶尿、细菌尿；见表6）。 图14. 可使用影像学检查如超声波来查找CKD的潜在原因，并排除肾后性氮质血症的原因，如(a) 输尿管结石或(b) 肾盂积水。图片由Lumbry Park兽医专家提供。 图15. 内源性CKD猫的典型超声表现，包括小而不规则的肾脏以及皮质髓质分界消失。图片由Lumbry Park兽医提供。
✜ Urine sediment examination An additional essential part of urinalysis in cats with suspected CKD is urine sediment examination, which provides clinically relevant information that may either identify the cause or highlight complications of the disease (eg, crystalluria, bacteriuria; Box 6). Figure 14. Imaging such as ultrasound can be used to look for underlying causes of CKD and exclude causes of postrenal azotaemia such as (a) ureterolithiasis or (b) hydronephrosis. Images courtesy of Lumbry Park Veterinary Specialists Figure 15. Typical ultrasound findings in a cat with intrinsic CKD, including a small, irregular kidney with loss of corticomedullary definition. 肾脏细胞学与组织病理学

Renal cytology and histopathology
当怀疑肾脏肿瘤或感染（例如猫传染性腹膜炎）时，建议进行肾脏细针穿刺活检。139 对于怀疑肾小球疾病的猫，应进行肾活检或尿液SDS-PAGE（十二烷基硫酸钠聚丙烯酰胺凝胶电泳）以评估ICGN。33 必须排除组织活检的禁忌症，如凝血功能障碍或难以控制的高血压，并且只有在潜在的诊断和治疗益处能够证明风险合理时才应进行该操作，其风险包括出血、肾积水和死亡。140 一旦诊断出CKD，肾脏取样的用途有限，因为预计存在典型的不可逆组织病理变化，这不会改变治疗方法。在怀疑ACKD或进展迅速的疾病且影像学异常提示存在其他病理（例如肿瘤）的情况下，可以考虑进行取样。
Fine-needle aspiration of the kidneys is indicated when neoplasia or infection (eg, feline infectious peritonitis) is suspected. 139 Kidney biopsy or SDS-PAGE (sodium dodecyl sulfate polyacrylamide gel electrophoresis) on urine is indicated in cats with suspected glomerular disease to assess for ICGN. 33 Contraindications to tissue biopsy such as coagulopathies or uncontrolled hypertension must be excluded, and the procedure should only be performed when the potential diagnostic and therapeutic benefits justify the risks, which include haemorrhage, hydronephrosis and death. 140 Renal sampling has limited utility once CKD has been diagnosed, as typical irreversible histopathological changes are expected and will not alter management approaches. Sampling may be considered in cases of suspected ACKD or rapidly progressive disease where imaging abnormalities are present suggesting additional pathology (eg, neoplasia).
肾损伤生物标志物的研究在人类和兽医医学中正在迅速扩展，这主要是由于目前使用的肾功能标志物的局限性。血清肌酐和SDMA是肾小球滤过率（GFR）的替代标志物，因此在肾功能没有同时下降的情况下对肾损伤不敏感。最初对新型生物标志物的研究主要集中在急性肾损伤（AKI）的早期检测，多项研究已经表明，损伤生物标志物能够在功能性标志物出现升高前数天识别AKI。在这些研究中，一些慢性肾病（CKD）动物被作为对照组，出乎意料的是，许多表面上稳定的CKD个体显示出损伤生物标志物浓度升高，这表明存在持续的肾小管上皮损伤或应激。在对1期CKD犬的初步研究中，已经显示肾小管上皮损伤标志物与疾病进展速度之间存在关联；然而，目前在CKD猫（特别是IRIS 1期）中缺乏可比数据。
Research into biomarkers of kidney injury is rapidly expanding in both human and veterinary medicine, largely driven by the limitations of currently used markers of renal function. Serum creatinine and SDMA are surrogate markers of GFR and, as such, are insensitive to renal injury where there is no concurrent decline in kidney function. 91 Initial investigations into novel biomarkers primarily focused on the early detection of AKI, and multiple studies have demonstrated that injury biomarkers can identify AKI days before increases in functional markers are observed. 106 In several of these studies, animals with CKD were included as control groups and, unexpectedly, many individuals with apparently stable CKD exhibited increased concentrations of injury biomarkers, suggesting the presence of ongoing tubular epithelial damage or stress. 141 In preliminary studies in dogs with stage 1 CKD, an association between markers of tubular epithelial injury and the rate of disease progression has been shown; 142 however, comparable data in cats with CKD (particularly IRIS stage 1) are currently lacking.
血清胱抑素C是一种由有核细胞产生的蛋白质，在肾小球中可自由滤过，然后在近曲小管中被重吸收。它曾被提出作为猫慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）的有用生物标志物，但数据显示其与肾小球滤过率（GFR）之间仅有弱相关性，因此结论认为其并无实用价值。 143 目前仍需确定肾小管上皮损伤的生物标志物（如胱抑素B）或其他反映肾内病理生理过程的标志物，是否足够敏感用于早期慢性肾病（CKD）的诊断。早期阶段的诊断具有重要的临床意义，但同时也是一大挑战，因为现有的诊断方法通常只能在大量肾单位已经损失之后才识别CKD。通过敏感的生物标志物更早地识别CKD，可能有助于在肾功能不可逆丧失之前进行更有效的治疗干预。 141 FGF23是一种由骨细胞和成骨细胞产生的激素，负责调节磷和活性维生素D（见框2）。
Serum cystatin C is a protein produced by nucleated cells and filtered freely at the glomerulus before reabsorption in the proximal tubules. It was proposed as a useful biomarker for CKD in cats, but data showed only a weak correlation between GFR and serum cystatin C and, hence, it was concluded not to be useful. 143 It also remains to be determined whether biomarkers of tubular epithelial injury (eg, cystatin B), or other markers reflecting intrarenal pathophysiological processes, are sufficiently sensitive for the diagnosis of early-stage CKD. This stage represents a significant diagnostic challenge, yet is of critical clinical importance, as current diagnostic approaches often identify CKD only after substantial nephron loss has already occurred. Earlier identification of CKD through sensitive biomarkers might enable more effective therapeutic intervention before irreversible loss of kidney function has developed. 141 FGF23 is a hormone produced by osteocytes and osteoblasts that is responsible for regulation of phosphate and calcitriol (see Box 2).
研究表明，患有氮质血症性慢性肾病（CKD）的猫，其血清FGF23浓度显著高于健康猫；此外，随着CKD严重程度的增加，FGF23浓度显著升高。FGF23是CKD中磷代谢紊乱的生物标志物，可用于识别可能从干预中受益的猫，但不建议将其作为早期诊断该疾病的筛查检测。
Serum FGF23 concentrations have been shown to be significantly higher in cats with azotaemic CKD than in healthy cats; moreover, concentrations increased significantly with increasing severity of CKD.71,144 FGF23 is a biomarker for phosphate derangement in CKD, identifying cats who may benefit from intervention, but it is not recommended as a screening test for early diagnosis of the disease. 145

CKD的IRIS分期

IRIS staging of CKD
IRIS 分期标准化了慢性肾脏疾病 (CKD) 严重程度的评估，指导循证治疗和监测决策，并改善了临床医生和护理人员之间的沟通和预后评估。它基于空腹血清肌酐浓度或肌酐和 SDMA（图16）。随后的子分期基于肾蛋白尿和收缩压（SBP）的程度，这些都是可通过治疗干预而改变的疾病进展风险因子。图16. 国际肾脏兴趣学会 (IRIS) 慢性肾脏疾病 (CKD) 分期和子分期的决策流程。SBP = 收缩压（SBP）；SDMA = 对称二甲基精氨酸（SDMA）；UPC = 尿蛋白:肌酐比值
IRIS staging standardises assessment of the severity of CKD, guides evidence-based treatment and monitoring decisions, and improves communication and prognostication for the benefit of clinicians and caregivers alike. It is based on either fasting serum creatinine concentration alone or creatinine and SDMA (Figure 16). Subsequent substaging is based on the magnitude of renal proteinuria and SBP, which are therapeutically modifiable risk factors for disease progression. Figure 16. Decision-making for International Renal Interest Society (IRIS) staging and substaging of chronic kidney disease (CKD). SBP = systolic blood pressure; SDMA = symmetric dimethylarginine; UPC = urine protein:creatinine ratio

何时进行IRIS分期

When to perform IRIS staging
✜ IRIS 分期应仅在已确诊为慢性肾病（CKD）的猫进行，基于持续功能性或慢性结构性变化的证据（参见“慢性肾病（CKD）的诊断”）。 ✜ IRIS 分期应仅限于临床稳定的 CKD 猫。对于急性肾损伤（AKI）或急性加慢性肾病（CKD）（ACKD）猫，或者当肌酐或 SDMA 浓度异常时，不适合进行分期。
✜ IRIS staging should only be performed in a cat with a confirmed diagnosis of CKD, based on evidence of persistent functional or chronic structural changes (see ‘Making a diagnosis of CKD’). ✜ IRIS staging should be restricted to cats with clinically stable CKD. Staging is not appropriate in cats with AKI or ACKD, or when creatinine or SDMA concentrations are

如何进行 IRIS 分期

How to perform IRIS staging
快速变化（例如，脱水、上尿路或下尿路梗阻，或近期液体治疗）。✜ 应在分期前纠正肾前性和肾后性氮质血症的原因。IRIS 建议仅使用空腹血清肌酐，或肌酐和 SDMA，在临床稳定且水合良好的患者身上至少测量两次。如果浓度不一致，应在 2–4 周后重复测量，然后再确认分期。✜ 初始分期（IRIS 1-4 期） 在确诊慢性肾病（CKD）后，根据空腹肌酐或肌酐和 SDMA 的测量结果，在稳定且水合充分的猫中分配 IRIS 期，并确保至少两次测量结果一致（表 1）。1,69 如果肌酐和 SDMA 对 CKD 分期存在差异，IRIS 建议在 2–4 周内重复测量。如果差异仍然存在，则分配更晚期的 CKD 分期是适当的。69 ✜ 细分 IRIS 分类应根据蛋白尿的程度进行细分，并参考表 1。
rapidly changing (eg, with dehydration, upper or lower urinary tract obstruction, or recent fluid therapy). ✜ Prerenal and postrenal causes of azotaemia should be corrected before staging. IRIS recommends using fasting serum creatinine alone, or creatinine and SDMA, measured on at least two occasions in a well-hydrated, clinically stable patient. If concentrations are inconsistent, measurement(s) should be repeated after 2–4 weeks before staging is confirmed. ✜ Initial staging (IRIS stages 1-4) Following a confirmed diagnosis of CKD, assign an IRIS stage based on fasting creatinine alone, or creatinine and SDMA, in a stable, hydrated cat, ensuring consistent results on at least two occasions (Table 1).1,69 If discrepancies between creatinine and SDMA exist regarding the CKD stage, IRIS recommends repeating the measurements within 2–4 weeks. If the discrepancy persists, assigning the more advanced stage of CKD would be an appropriate response. 69 ✜ Substaging Substage the IRIS classification according to the degree of proteinuria and the Table 1.

– 蛋白尿 – 非蛋白尿、边缘蛋白尿或蛋白尿的一个亚阶段（表2）是基于尿蛋白/肌酐比值（UPC）来确定的（理想情况下，应至少在两次相隔至少2周采集的尿样中测定）。62,69,146,147 – 收缩压（SBP） – 正常血压、升高前期高血压、高血压或重度高血压的一个亚阶段（表2）是基于多次连续SBP测量来确定的。例外情况是如果已经存在肾外靶器官损伤，则应立即进行治疗（见“CKD管理”）。1,69 ✜ 重新评估与监测 如果肌酐/SDMA浓度、蛋白尿或SBP出现显著变化，或猫的治疗方案或临床状况发生改变，应重复分期过程。
– Proteinuria – a substage of non-proteinuric, borderline proteinuric or proteinuric (Table 2) is assigned based on UPC (ideally, measured on a minimum of two urine samples collected at least 2 weeks apart).62,69,146,147 – SBP – a substage of normotensive, prehypertensive, hypertensive or severely hypertensive (Table 2) is assigned based on multiple sequential SBP measurements. The exception is if extra-renal target organ damage is already present, in which case immediate treatment is indicated (see ‘Management of CKD’).1,69 ✜ Re-evaluation and monitoring Repeat the staging process if there is a significant change in creatinine/SDMA concentrations, proteinuria or SBP, or a change in the cat’s treatment regimen or clinical status.

慢性肾病（CKD）的管理

Management of CKD
总体来说，慢性肾病（CKD）管理的目标是处理临床症状，以优化患病猫的生活质量，并实施旨在减缓疾病进展的措施，同时与照护者合作，确保干预措施适合个体情况（图17）。78,148 当猫进入后期IRIS阶段时，前者目标最为重要，而后者措施在早期IRIS阶段更为重要。在开具药物时应始终考虑依从性；多种药物的使用是常见情况，可能影响CKD猫的生活质量149，并增加“照护者负担”（框7）。80 图17。
In broad terms, the aims of management of CKD are to address clinical signs to optimise the quality of life of the affected cat and to implement measures aimed at slowing progression of the disease, all the while working as a team with the caregiver to ensure interventions are appropriate for the individual situation (Figure 17).78,148 The former aims assume most importance as the cat reaches the later IRIS stages, whereas the latter measures are more important in the earlier IRIS stages. Compliance should always be considered when prescribing medications; a requirement for multiple drugs is commonplace and can impact quality of life in cats with CKD, 149 as well as contributing to ‘caregiver burden’ (Box 7). 80 Figure 17.
管理患有慢性肾病（CKD）猫的因素。ACE = 血管紧张素转换酶；ARB = 血管紧张素受体拮抗剂；Ca:P = 钙:磷比；DJD = 退行性关节病；PEG = 聚乙二醇；SC = 皮下；UPC = 尿蛋白:肌酐比；UTI = 泌尿道感染
Factors to consider in the management of cats with chronic kidney disease (CKD). ACE = angiotensin-converting enzyme; ARB = angiotensin receptor blocker; Ca:P = calcium:phosphorus ratio; DJD = degenerative joint disease; PEG = polyethylene glycol; SC = subcutaneous; UPC = urine protein:creatinine ratio; UTI = urinary tract infection

饮食干预

Dietary intervention
有强有力的证据支持对患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫进行饮食调整。喂食兽医治疗肾脏饮食可以延长生存期并降低尿毒症发作的风险。66,151 延缓CKD进展并延长生存期的主要关注营养素是膳食磷（包括有机磷和无机磷）；通常，肾脏饮食提供约80–130 mg磷/100 kcal，而非肾脏饮食可能含量高达400–600 mg/100 kcal。高磷血症与CKD猫的生存期缩短有关。70,146,147 图18. 将护理者纳入对其猫的监测——例如，通过鼓励在家评估体重并保持日记——可以帮助护理者了解猫的病情状况，并促进兽医诊所与护理者之间的沟通。图片由Samantha Taylor提供。在一项研究中，血清磷每增加0.32 mmol/l（1 mg/dl），死亡风险增加11.8%。
There is strong evidence to support dietary modification in cats with CKD. Feeding a veterinary therapeutic renal diet can prolong survival and reduce the risk of uraemic crises.66,151 The primary nutrient of concern to slow the progress of CKD and prolong survival is dietary phosphorus (both organic and inorganic); typically, renal diets provide ~80–130 mg phosphorus/100 kcal, whereas non-renal diets may contain up to 400–600 mg/100 kcal. Hyperphosphataemia is associated with reduced survival in cats with CKD.70,146,147 Figure 18. Including caregivers in the monitoring of their cat – for example, by encouraging assessment of body weight at home and keeping a diary – can help caregivers feel informed about the status of their cat’s illness and encourage communication between the veterinary clinic and caregiver. Image courtesy of Samantha Taylor In one study, for every 0.32 mmol/l (1 mg/dl) increase in serum phosphate, there was an 11.8% increased risk of death.
70 血浆磷酸盐浓度是 FGF23 的强效刺激因子，FGF23 是一种参与 CKD-MBD 发展的磷调素（见图 5）。152 为患有 CKD 的猫喂食低磷饮食可降低血清磷和 FGF23。153 图 5. CKD-MBD 的病理生理机制。肾小球滤过率（GFR）降低导致磷的滤过和排泄减少。磷增加刺激成纤维细胞生长因子（FGF-23） 23（FGF23）分泌，FGF23 尝试增加肾脏磷排泄，但由于肾单位数量减少而受限。FGF23 降低钙三醇浓度，而磷与钙复合增加，导致离子化低钙血症。血清磷增加以及离子化钙（iCa）和钙三醇下降都刺激甲状旁腺激素（PTH）分泌。
70 Plasma phosphate concentration is a potent stimulator of FGF23, a phosphatonin involved in the development of CKD-MBD (see Figure 5). 152 Feeding a reduced-phosphorus diet decreases serum phosphate and FGF23 in cats with CKD. 153 Figure 5. Pathophysiology of CKD-MBD. A reduced glomerular filtration rate (GFR) leads to reduced filtration and excretion of phosphate. Increasing phosphate stimulates fibroblast growth factor 23 (FGF23) secretion, which attempts to increase renal phosphate excretion but is limited by decreasing nephron numbers. FGF23 reduces calcitriol concentrations, while increased phosphate complexes with calcium, causing ionised hypocalcaemia. Increased serum phosphate and reduced ionised calcium (iCa) and calcitriol all stimulate parathyroid hormone (PTH) secretion.
这些慢性的生化变化会导致软组织矿化，包括肾钙沉着、肾性骨营养不良、慢性肾病（CKD）进展以及存活时间缩短。肾脏饮食对患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫也可能有其他益处；例如，补充欧米伽3脂肪酸，如二十碳五烯酸（EPA）。在一项研究中，EPA含量最高的肾脏饮食在延长患慢性肾病（CKD）猫的存活期方面最为有效。额外的益处包括补充抗氧化剂、钾和B族维生素；这些饮食还可能含有碱化成分。在为个别猫选择特定的低磷饮食时，应进行营养评估，包括测量体重、体况评分（BCS）和肌肉状况评分（MCS），并识别猫的偏好（口味、质地、喂食频率）以及相关的照顾者因素（经济状况、生活方式）。其他需要考虑的因素如下所述。
These chronic biochemical changes lead to soft tissue mineralisation including nephrocalcinosis, renal osteodystrophy, CKD progression and reduced survival times Renal diets may also have other benefits for cats with CKD; for example, supplementation with omega 3 fatty acids such as eicosapentaenoic acid (EPA). In one study, the renal diet with the highest EPA content was the most effective in extending survival in cats with CKD. 67 Additional benefits include supplementation with antioxidants, potassium and B vitamins; they may also contain alkalinising ingredients. When it comes to choosing a specific reduced phosphate diet for an individual cat, a nutritional assessment should be performed to include measurement of body weight, BCS and MCS, and to identify cat preferences (flavour, texture, frequency of feeding) as well as relevant caregiver factors (finances, lifestyle). 154 Other considerations are outlined below.
✜ 喂食罐装或湿食可以为猫提供更多的水分，有助于维持适当的水合状态。 ✜ 罐装食品的热量密度较低，猫需要摄入更大量的食物，因此一些猫可能难以维持体重。相比之下，干粮或在给患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫引入新饮食时，最好在至少 1–2 周内将少量新食物与现有食物逐步混合。同时提供新旧食物可能更易被接受，因为猫通常不喜欢将新旧食物混合。重要的是，绝不要在疾病期间或例如猫在兽医诊所进行诊断检查时引入新饮食，因为它们可能会产生食物厌恶，从而影响长期接受那种食物。
✜ Feeding a canned or moist diet will provide more water to the cat, which can aid in maintaining adequate hydration. ✜ Canned diets will be lower in caloric density, requiring cats to eat a greater volume of food, and it may thus be challenging for some cats to maintain weight. By contrast, kibble or When introducing a new diet to a cat with CKD, it is best to offer small amounts of the new food gradually over at least 1–2 weeks alongside the existing food. Cats often do not like having the new and existing diets mixed, so offering the foods side by side may be better tolerated. It is important never to introduce a novel diet during a period of illness or while, for example, a cat is undergoing diagnostic investigations within the veterinary clinic, as they could develop food aversions affecting long-term acceptance of the food.
针对早期肾病的饮食通常比针对晚期肾病的饮食提供略多的蛋白质。最终，是否选择‘早期’或‘晚期’肾脏饮食的决定可能取决于猫的IRIS分期之外的其他因素。例如，如果猫摄入的热量少于理想值，那么选择高蛋白饮食可能更可取，以确保猫的蛋白质需求得到满足。此外，许多‘早期’肾脏饮食提供略多的磷，并且可能具有较低的钙：磷（Ca:P）比值；然而，这并非普遍情况。在慢性肾病（CKD）管理中，应优先通过维持足够的热量摄入来保持健康的体重、体况评分（BCS）和肌肉状况评分（MCS）。一些猫只需摄入其静息能量需求（RER；计算公式为70 × 体重[kg]^0.75）即可维持体重。然而，对于其他猫，RER需要乘以约1.2–1.4的维持能量需求（MER）系数。
Diets marketed for early kidney disease typically provide slightly more protein than diets marketed for more advanced disease. Ultimately, the decision of whether to feed an ‘early’ or ‘advanced’ renal diet may be based on factors other than the cat’s assigned IRIS stage. For example, if a cat is eating fewer calories than ideal, then feeding a higher protein diet may be preferable to ensure the cat’s protein requirement is being met. Additionally, many of the ‘early’ renal diets provide slightly more phosphate and may have a lower calcium:phosphorus (Ca:P) ratio; however, this is not universally the case. Preserving a healthy body weight, BCS and MCS through maintaining an adequate caloric intake should be prioritised in the management of CKD. Some cats will maintain weight simply by eating their resting energy requirement (RER; calculated as 70 x body weight [kg]0.75). However, for others the RER will need to be multiplied by a maintenance energy requirement (MER) factor of approximately 1.2–1.4.
如果猫咪所需的能量超过 2.0 倍基础能量需求（RER），则应进行额外的诊断以评估是否存在合并疾病（例如，甲状腺功能亢进、慢性肠病、外分泌胰腺功能不全）。虽然理想情况下应专门喂食治疗性肾脏饮食，但这并不总是可能的，应鼓励照护者即使肾脏饮食只占摄入热量的一部分，也持续喂食肾脏饮食。一种肾脏饮食占 50% 或更多的饮食可以对生存产生有益影响。66
If a cat requires more than 2.0 x RER, additional diagnostics should be performed to assess for comorbid conditions (eg, hyperthyroidism, chronic enteropathy, exocrine pancreatic insufficiency). Although, ideally, a therapeutic renal diet would be fed exclusively, this is not always possible, and caregivers should be encouraged to continue to feed the renal diet even if it only makes up a proportion of the calories eaten. A diet that is 50% or more renal diet can have a beneficial effect on survival. 66

高磷血症的进一步管理

Further management of hyperphosphataemia
给予磷限制的肾脏饮食是维持血清磷浓度在国际肾脏疾病学会（IRIS）推荐的各阶段CKD目标范围内的首选初始治疗方法。干粮通常每克提供的热量约为大多数罐装或新鲜饮食的四倍。✜ CKD猫的食欲可能时有波动；因此，通常希望有多种适当的饮食选择以满足营养需求（表3）。有证据表明，这些饮食可以减缓CKD进展并延长生存时间。66–68,151 表3. 按CKD的IRIS阶段的目标血清磷浓度
Feeding a phosphate-restricted renal diet is the initial treatment of choice to maintain serum phosphate concentrations within the target range recommended by IRIS for each stage of CKD dry diets will typically provide about four times more calories per gram than most canned or fresh diets. ✜ Cats with CKD can have a waxing and waning appetite; hence, it is often desirable to have multiple appropriate diet options that meet nutritional needs. 1 N/A 2 0.81–1.45 (2.5–4.5) 3 0.81–1.63 (2.5–5.0) 4 0.81–1.94 (2.5–6.0) CKD = chronic kidney disease; IRIS = International Renal Interest Society; N/A = not However, if feeding a renal diet is not sufficient to reduce serum phosphate to within the target range after 4–6 weeks, a phosphate binder can be considered to help reduce phosphate further (Figures 19 and 20). Phosphate binders should be thoroughly mixed into all meals fed to the cat, with the total daily dose divided between the number of meals fed. This is because their efficacy requires simultaneous administration of dietary phosphate and the binding compound. Calciumcontaining phosphate binders (eg, calcium carbonate ± chitosan) are effective and relatively cheap, but these should be avoided in hypercalcaemic cats. Alternative, non-calcium-containing phosphate binders include aluminium hydroxide, sevelamer and lanthanum carbonate.

高钙血症的管理

Management of hypercalcaemia
肾脏疾病通常被认为是猫血钙离子升高的三大病因之一（其他常见原因是特发性高钙血症和肿瘤）。155,156 一项回顾性队列研究发现，在患有氮质血症性慢性肾病（CKD）的猫中，大约 20% 在诊断时表现出血钙离子升高，另外 26% 在诊断后 12 个月内发展为明显或持续的血钙离子升高。157 大约一半的氮质血症性 CKD 猫在开始磷限制肾脏饮食后，钙浓度会随时间增加。112 血钙离子升高在 CKD 猫中的后果是增加肾钙沉积的风险，这似乎与 CKD 的进一步进展相关。54 对于一些猫来说，喂食磷限制饮食会通过 PTH 非依赖机制诱发血钙离子升高；157 因此，改为较高磷饮食可以使升高的血钙离子浓度恢复正常。
Kidney disease is routinely cited as one of the top three aetiologies associated with ionised hypercalcaemia in cats (other common causes are idiopathic hypercalcaemia and neopla- sia).155,156 A retrospective cohort study found that, of cats with azotaemic CKD, approximately 20% demonstrated ionised hypercal-caemia at diagnosis, and an additional 26% went on to develop marked or persistent ionised hypercalcaemia within 12 months of diagnosis. 157 Approximately half of all cats with azotaemic CKD will demonstrate increasing calcium concentrations over time after transition onto a phosphate-restricted renal diet. 112 The consequence of ionised hypercal-caemia in cats with CKD is an increased risk of nephrocalcinosis, which appears to be associated with more progressive CKD. 54 For some cats, feeding a phosphate-restricted diet induces ionised hypercalcaemia via PTH- independent mechanisms; 157 hence, transition onto a higher phosphate diet can normalise elevated iCa concentrations.
在一项研究中，导致猫出现高钙血症的饮食与高钙血症得到缓解的饮食之间的关键区别是饮食中的钙磷比：钙磷比为1.9:1会引起高钙血症，而钙磷比为1.3:1则会使离子化钙恢复正常。因此，管理患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫高钙血症的一种新方法是结合这两个营养目标——即喂食钙含量≤200毫克/100千卡且钙磷比<1.4:1的饮食。在最近的一项病例系列研究中，这种方法被发现可改善9/10只患有慢性肾病（CKD）或特发性高钙血症的猫的离子化高钙血症。理想情况下，应在开始限制饮食磷之前，对所有新诊断慢性肾病（CKD）猫的钙和磷浓度进行仔细评估。不幸的是，总钙与离子化钙浓度之间的相关性较差，尤其是在患有氮质血症的慢性肾病（CKD）猫中。总钙会低估离子化钙浓度，因此，尽可能时，测量离子化钙是评估高钙血症的首选方法。
In one study, the key difference between diets fed to cats who developed hypercalcaemia vs those in whom hypercalcaemia resolved was the dietary Ca:P ratio: a Ca:P ratio of 1.9:1 induced hypercal-caemia and a ratio of 1.3:1 resulted in normalisation of iCa. 158 As such, a newer approach to manage hypercalcaemia in cats with CKD combines these two nutritional goals – namely, feeding a diet with ⩽200 mg calcium/100 kcal and a Ca:P ratio <1.4:1. In a recent case series, this approach was found to improve ionised hypercalcaemia in 9/10 cats with either CKD or idiopathic hypercalcaemia. 159 Ideally, the calcium and phosphate concentrations of all cats with newly diagnosed CKD should be carefully assessed prior to initiating dietary phosphate restriction. Unfortunately, there is a poor correlation between total calcium and iCa concentrations, particularly in cats with azotaemic CKD.157,160 Total calcium underestimates iCa concentrations and, as a result, measurement of iCa is the preferred method for assessment of hypercalcaemia, whenever possible.
另外，如果总钙浓度处于实验室参考范围的高端，应关注离子钙水平。如果猫在慢性肾病（CKD）诊断时或在开始治疗性肾脏饮食后出现高钙血症，或被诊断为草酸钙尿石，建议仔细选择一种替代的治疗性肾脏饮食，该饮食需满足每100千卡钙不超过200毫克且钙磷比<1.4:1的标准。重要的是要记住，特定饮食的营养成分经常变化（频率可为每6–12个月一次）；因此，如果记录到钙意外增加，联系制造商可能是明智的。已提出通过补充肾脏饮食中的镁来减少离子高钙血症的倾向，并稳定血清FGF23，以作为慢性肾病（CKD）相关矿物质骨病（CKD-MBD）管理的一部分；因此，将镁添加到肾脏食物中可能对在肾脏饮食下出现高钙血症的猫的管理有用，从而允许对磷进行更严格的限制。
Alternatively, a total calcium concentration at the high end of the laboratory RI should raise concern for ionised If a cat is hypercalcaemic at diagnosis of CKD or following the introduction of a therapeutic renal diet, or is diagnosed with calcium oxalate uroliths, careful selection of an alternative therapeutic renal diet that meets the criteria of ⩽200 mg calcium/100 kcal and a Ca:P ratio <1.4:1 is recommended. It is important to remember that the nutrient profiles of specific diets evolve frequently (as often as every 6–12 months); hence, contacting manufacturers may be advisable if unexpected increases in calcium are documented. Magnesium supplementation of renal diets has been proposed to reduce the tendency for ionised hypercalcaemia and to stabilise serum FGF23 as part of the management of CKD-MBD;64,161 hence, the addition of magnesium to renal foods may be useful in managing cats who develop hypercalcaemia when fed renal diets, allowing greater phosphate restriction.
对于高钙血症的猫，也可以考虑补充奇亚籽，无论是与上述营养方案结合使用，还是单独使用。奇亚籽可以在浸泡20分钟后混入湿食中，或者浸泡过夜，因此还能额外增加水分摄入。建议剂量为每只猫每24小时1–2克，分次混入每餐。其他选项包括双膦酸盐，以及一种新型治疗药物西那卡塞（cinacalcet），这是一种钙敏感受体激动剂，可上调甲状旁腺上的钙感受器，已用于治疗人类慢性肾病（CKD）相关的高钙血症。关于其在猫慢性肾病（CKD）中的使用信息有限，但已有成功管理特发性高钙血症的报道。
Supplementation with chia seeds may also be considered for hypercalcaemic cats, either in conjunction with the above nutritional approach or on its own.159,162 Chia seeds can be mixed with wet food after being soaked for 20 mins, or alternatively overnight, and therefore contribute additionally to water intake. A suggested dosage is 1–2 g/cat/q24h divided and mixed into each meal.159,162 Other options include bisphosphonates, 163 and a novel therapeutic, cinacalcet, which is a calcimimetic that upregulates the calcium-sensing receptor on the parathyroid gland and has been used to treat hypercalcaemia of CKD in humans. 164 There is limited information on its use in feline CKD, but successful management of idiopathic hypercalcaemia has been reported. 165

恶心和食欲减退的管理

Management of nausea and hyporexia
患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫可能会因尿毒素（见框 3）影响中枢化学感受器触发区而导致恶心、呕吐和食欲不振，以及由于肾排泄减少而引起的食欲抑制物质积累而导致的食欲调节异常。这些临床症状在高阶段 CKD 的猫中更为常见；因此，当在 IRIS 一级或二级 CKD 猫中观察到这些症状时，应考虑合并疾病。食欲不振对照护者来说是一个显著的生活质量问题，并且在 CKD 患猫中可能导致蛋白质和热量营养不良，进而造成体重下降以及体况评分（BCS）和肌肉评分（MCS）的减少。因此，一旦发现这种食欲减退，应积极进行管理，并处理任何可能导致食欲不振的 CKD 并发症，如脱水、便秘、低钾血症、酸中毒或贫血。应考虑使用中枢作用的止吐药，如马洛匹坦、米氮平和昂丹司琼进行管理。
Cats with CKD can suffer from nausea, vomiting and inappetence as a result of uraemic toxins (see Box 3) affecting the central chemoreceptor trigger zone, as well as appetite dysregulation due to the accumulation of anorexigenic substances secondary to decreased renal excretion. 83 These clinical signs are more prevalent in cats with higher stage CKD; 166 hence, when observed in cats with IRIS stage 1 or 2 CKD, comorbidities should be considered. Inappetence is a significant quality of life concern for caregivers, 149 and in the CKD patient may result in protein and calorie malnutrition, with subsequent losses in body weight and reductions in BCS and MCS. 167 A reduced appetite should, therefore, be actively managed as soon as such losses are appreciated, and any complications of CKD that can contribute to inappetence, such as dehydration, constipation, hypokalaemia, acidosis or anaemia, should be addressed. Centrally acting antiemetics, such as maropitant, mirtazapine and ondansetron,168–170 should be considered for management.
在针对患有 IRIS 2 或 3 期 CKD 的猫的安慰剂对照试验中，马洛匹坦（口服给药 2 周）被证明可以减少呕吐，而米氮平（口服或经皮给药 3 周）不仅减少呕吐，还能增加食欲和体重。因此，米氮平是 CKD 猫营养管理中的有用辅助药物。根据临床经验，剂量可以根据效果进行调整（例如，每 48 小时给药一次）以刺激食欲并避免副作用，长期使用也很常见。生长激素受体激动剂卡普莫雷林是管理 CKD 伴随食欲调节异常的合理选择。报道表明，猫在接受卡普莫雷林治疗时可能会出现短暂性心动过缓和低血压等副作用，因此需要进行适当监测，并且不建议对血流动力学不稳定的患者使用该药。
In placebo-controlled trials of cats with IRIS stage 2 or 3 CKD, maropitant (given orally for 2 weeks) was shown to reduce vomiting, 170 and mirtazapine (given orally or transdermally for 3 weeks) reduced vomiting and also increased appetite and body weight.169,171 Mirtazapine, therefore, is a useful adjunct to the nutritional management of cats with CKD. Based on anecdotal experience, dosage can be titrated to effect (eg, administering q48h) to stimulate appetite and avoid side effects, and long-term administration is often utilised. The ghrelin receptor agonist capromorelin is a logical choice for managing the appetite dysregulation that accompanies CKD. 172 Transient bradycardia and hypotension have been reported as side effects of capromorelin treatment in cats; thus, appropriate monitoring is required, and the drug is not recommended for haemodynamically compromised patients.
根据轶事，有报告称组胺H2受体拮抗剂（例如法莫替丁）或质子泵抑制剂（例如奥美拉唑）能够缓解一些猫慢性肾脏病（CKD）患者的食欲不振；然而，胃酸过多的存在与程度，以及这些药物的疗效仍未得到证实。最新的证据表明，高促胃素血症和胃溃疡的发生率低于之前的假设。如果考虑对猫进行抗酸治疗，奥美拉唑优于法莫替丁，因为法莫替丁在使用几天后效果会下降。然而，鉴于给宠物管理多种药物所带来的照护负担，处方应仅限于对胃十二指肠溃疡有强烈怀疑或已确诊的病例。表4提供了用于治疗CKD猫呕吐、恶心及食欲不振的常用药物剂量信息。
Anecdotally, there are reports of histamine H2 receptor antagonists (eg, famotidine) or proton pump inhibitors (eg, omeprazole) alleviating inappetence in some feline CKD patients; however, the presence and degree of gastric hyperacidity, and efficacy of these medications remain unproven. Recent evidence indicates that hypergastrinaemia and gastric ulceration are less common than previously assumed.173,174 If therapy for hyperacidity in cats is considered, omeprazole is superior to famotidine, as the latter loses efficacy after a couple of days. 175 However, given the caregiver burden associated with administering multiple medications, prescribing should be reserved for cases with a strong suspicion of, or confirmed, gastroduodenal ulceration. 176 Table 4 provides dosage information for medications commonly used to treat nausea, vomiting and inappetence in cats with CKD.
对于持续食欲不振、较高阶段慢性肾病（CKD）和/或在急性慢性肾病（CKD）加重（ACKD）发作期间的猫而言，使用喂养管（例如食管造口管）可能是有益的；喂养管还可帮助维持水分和投药。在“2022年ISFM住院猫食欲不振管理共识指南”中提供了关于如何放置喂养管的信息和相关视频。表4. 在患有CKD的猫中常用的止吐药、食欲促进剂和抗酸药*
A feeding tube (eg, oesophagostomy tube) may be beneficial in cats with persistent hyporexia, higher stage CKD and/or during episodes of ACKD;167,178 a tube can also assist with maintaining hydration and administering medications. Information and accompanying videos on how to place feeding tubes are provided in the ‘2022 ISFM consensus guidelines on management of the inappetent hospitalised cat’. 167 Table 4. 脱水的管理和预防

Management and prevention of dehydration
由于慢性肾病（CKD）患者的尿液浓缩能力受损，脱水在他们中间很常见，可能导致厌食、嗜睡、虚弱、便秘以及尿毒症危象的易感性增加。脱水可能引发对肾脏有负面影响的病理生理反应。临床上出现脱水的猫可能受益于住院及静脉输液治疗以纠正缺失，然后在家中进行干预以增加其自愿饮水量。iCatCare 的资源《鼓励您的猫喝水：照顾者指南》（可在 icatcare.org/cat-advice/cat-carer-guides 获取）描述了多种增加饮水量的方法。
Dehydration is common in CKD patients due to their impaired ability to concentrate urine, and can lead to inappetence, lethargy, weakness, constipation and an increased susceptibility to uraemic crises. 166 Dehydration may precipitate pathophysiological responses that have a negative effect on the kidney. Cats with clinical dehydration may benefit from hospitalisation and intravenous fluid therapy to correct deficits, followed by home interventions to increase their voluntary water An iCatCare resource entitled ‘Encouraging your cat to drink: a guide for caregivers’ (available at icatcare.org/cat-advice/cat-carer-guides) describes various ways in which water intake can be increased.
这些包括：✜ 提供多个水源的免费获取，包括饮水机和宽边陶瓷碗，并将它们放置在猫咪容易接近的位置（图21a）；✜ 将水碗提高，使患有退行性关节疾病（DJD）的猫咪不需要低头喝水（图21b）；✜ 喂食湿食（考虑到需要谨慎过渡，以及湿食每克热量较低）；✜ 在食物中添加水（注意不要对猫咪的热量或营养摄入产生不利影响）；✜ 使用营养强化水产品；这些补充剂已被证明可以提高脱水猫咪的自愿饮水量和水合状态。179 图21。
These include: ✜ Providing free access to multiple water sources, including water fountains and widebrimmed ceramic bowls, positioned in easily accessible locations for the cat (Figure 21a); ✜ Raising water bowls so that cats with DJD do not need to lower their head to drink (Figure 21b); ✜ Feeding a wet diet (taking into consideration the need for a careful transition, as well as the lower calorie content per gram in wet foods); ✜ Adding water to the food (taking care not to adversely affect the cat’s calorie or nutrient intake); ✜ Using nutrient-enriched water products; these supplements have been shown to improve voluntary water intake and hydration in dehydrated cats. 179 Figure 21.
采取增加自愿饮水量的干预措施，例如 (a) 鼓励使用饮水喷泉或 (b) 将水碗提高到更舒适的位置，对患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫有益。图片由 Samantha Taylor (a) 和 Sarah Collins (b) 提供。由照护者在家中进行皮下输液并不适用于所有 CKD 猫，但大多数猫可以接受，因此对于在采取上述措施后仍有持续脱水的猫，可以考虑进行此操作（图 22）。180 采用加温输液、提供零食以及高效操作等技术可以提高猫的耐受性。180 通常每次给予每只猫 75–100 毫升，每 1–3 天一次，猫可耐受良好。理想情况下使用低渗溶液（半浓度乳酸林格液、0.45% 生理盐水、Normosol-M）以减少钠负荷，但实际上猫对其他晶体液也能耐受且不会出现临床并发症。通过输液套件和蝴蝶针或标准针进行输液；对于钾缺乏的猫，可在液体中添加氯化钾（例如，每 1000 毫升液体中添加 20–30 mEq KCl）。180 图 22。
Interventions to increase voluntary water intake, such as (a) encouraging use of a water fountain or (b) raising the water bowl to a more comfortable position, can benefit cats with CKD. Images courtesy of Samantha Taylor (a) and Sarah Collins (b) Administration of subcutaneous fluids at home by caregivers is not recommended for all cats with CKD, but is well accepted by most cats and, hence, can be considered for those with ongoing dehydration despite the measures discussed above (Figure 22). 180 Techniques such as warming the fluids, offering treats and performing the procedure efficiently improve patient tolerance. 180 Giving 75–100 ml/cat every 1–3 days is generally well tolerated. Ideally, hypotonic solution (halfstrength lactated Ringer’s solution, 0.45% saline, Normosol-M) is used to reduce the sodium load, but, in practice, cats tolerate other crystalloid fluids without clinical complication. Fluid is administered via a giving set and butterfly or standard needle; potassium chloride can be added to the solution in deficient cats (eg, 20–30 mEq KCl per 1000 ml fluid). 180 Figure 22.

Subcutaneous fluid therapy. (a) The procedure for administering subcutaneous fluids should be demonstrated to caregivers for use in the home environment. (b) A cat receiving subcutaneous fluids at home using a giving set and fluid bag. Images courtesy of Sarah
