贫血的管理

Management of anaemia
肾脏负责产生红细胞生成素（EPO）这种激素，它刺激骨髓生成红细胞。随着慢性肾病（CKD）的进展，EPO的产生减少，从而可能导致贫血。贫血的后果包括虚弱、嗜睡、食欲不振，以及由于缺氧促进纤维化发展可能加速CKD进展。贫血还可能引起左心扩大，这可能使患者更容易发生心力衰竭和液体潴留。中度至重度贫血很可能会因为嗜睡和食欲不振而影响CKD猫的生活质量。在最近的一项研究中，贫血（PCV <27%）与CKD猫健康相关生活质量评分较低有关。因此，出于多种原因，贫血应在CKD患者中积极管理。
The kidney is responsible for producing the hormone erythropoietin (EPO), which stimulates the bone marrow to produce red blood cells. As CKD progresses, EPO production decreases and anaemia can result. Consequences of anaemia include weakness, lethargy, inappetence and potential acceleration of CKD progression due to hypoxia driving the development of fibrosis.63,181–183 Anaemia may also cause left heart enlargement, which could predispose patients to heart failure and fluid overload. 184 Moderate to severe anaemia likely has the potential to affect the quality of life of cats with CKD as a result of lethargy and inappetence. In a recent study, anaemia (PCV <27%) was associated with poorer health-related quality of life scores in cats with CKD. 149 Therefore, for multiple reasons, anaemia should be actively managed in CKD patients.
EPO缺乏可以通过人工制品如达贝泊汀α（如果无法获得，则使用重组人EPO）进行补充。达贝泊汀是EPO的长效形式，被认为比人EPO更少引起抗EPO抗体的产生。达贝泊汀的推荐起始剂量为每周皮下注射0.75–1 µg/kg，直到达到PCV参考下限（30%）。此后，根据需要将给药频率减至每两周一次，以维持足够的PCV（例如，⩾30%）（图23）。在诱导期，每次注射前应测量患者的收缩压（SBP）和PCV；一旦达到维持阶段，可以遵循常规监测计划（例如，每1–3个月检查一次SBP和PCV）。
EPO deficiency can be supplemented with artificial products such as darbepoetin alpha (or, if unavailable, recombinant human EPO). Darbepoetin is a longer acting form of EPO and is thought to give rise to fewer anti-EPO antibodies than human EPO. The recommended starting dosage for darbepoetin is 0.75–1 µg/kg subcutaneously once weekly until the low end of the PCV RI is reached (30%). 185 Thereafter, the dosing frequency is decreased to once every other week, as needed, to maintain an adequate PCV (eg, ⩾30%) (Figure 23). The patient’s SBP and PCV should be measured before each injection during the induction period; once maintenance has been reached, a regular monitoring schedule (eg, checking SBP and PCV every 1–3 months) can be followed.
图23. 采用促红细胞生成剂达比泊亭治疗慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）贫血的方法。IM = 肌肉注射; PCV = 红细胞压积（PCV）; SBP = 收缩压（SBP）; SC = 皮下注射。当开始EPO治疗时，建议进行铁补充（铁右旋糖酐 50 mg/只肌注每3–4周一次，硫酸亚铁 50–100 mg/只口服每天一次，富马酸亚铁 30–60 mg/只 [305 mg胶囊的五分之一] 每天一次），因为CKD猫往往存在相对铁缺乏。口服硫酸亚铁可能引起胃肠不适（尽管通常较轻微）；将剂量分开并随食物给药可减轻此副作用。需要注意的是，在慢性炎症和血清肝铁素浓度升高的情况下，口服铁的吸收将受到影响。缺氧诱导因子-脯氨酰羟化酶抑制剂（HIF-PHIs）已经在人体医学中使用了一段时间。它们被认为具有与达比泊亭相似甚至更好的疗效和安全性。
Figure 23. Approach to the treatment of anaemia of chronic kidney disease (CKD) with darbepoetin, an erythropoiesis-stimulating agent. IM = intramuscularly; PCV = packed cell volume; SBP = systolic blood pressure; SC = subcutaneously Iron supplementation (iron dextran 50 mg/cat IM q3–4 weeks, ferrous sulphate 50–100 mg/cat PO q24h, ferrous fumarate 30–60 mg/cat [one-fifth of a 305 mg capsule] q24h) is recommended when EPO therapy is initiated, as a relative iron deficiency is often present in cats with CKD. Oral ferrous sulphate can result in gastrointestinal upset (albeit usually mild); this side effect is reduced if the dose is divided and administered with food. Note that in the face of chronic inflammation and elevated serum hepcidin concentrations, absorption of orally administered iron will be compromised. 181 Hypoxia-inducible factor–prolyl hydroxylase inhibitors (HIF-PHIs) have been used in human medicine for some time. They are considered to have a similar, if not better, efficacy and safety profile than darbepoetin.
186 在一项针对健康成年猫的初步研究中，给予 Molidustat（一种 HIF-PHI 悬液）导致血清 EPO 浓度显著且剂量依赖性增加，并且与安慰剂对照的猫相比，血细胞比容（HCT）增加。187 在另一项研究中，21 只患有慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）的猫接受 Molidustat 或安慰剂治疗 28 天，接受药物的猫相比对照组血细胞比容（HCT）（PCV）显著增加。188 不良反应包括呕吐和红细胞增多症，其中在 CKD 猫中后者发生的可能性较小，推测由于 CKD 中存在多种抑制红细胞生成的反调节机制。188 Molidustat 的推荐剂量为 5 mg/kg 口服，每 24 小时一次，连续 28 天，在需要时可在停药 1 周后重新开始（图 24）。189 图 24。
186 Molidustat (an HIF-PHI suspension) given to healthy adult cats in a pilot study resulted in a significant and dose-dependent increase in serum EPO concentrations, and an increase in haematocrit, in comparison with placebo-treated cats. 187 In another study, where 21 cats with CKD were treated with molidustat or placebo for 28 days, cats who received the drug had a significant increase in PCV compared with the control group. 188 Adverse effects include vomiting and poly-cythaemia, with the latter less likely in cats with CKD, presumably due to the many counter-regulatory mechanisms suppressing red blood cell production in CKD. 188 The recommended dosage for molidustat is 5 mg/kg PO q24h for 28 days, restarting after a 1-week pause, as needed (Figure 24). 189 Figure 24.
慢性肾病（CKD）贫血的莫利杜司他（molidustat）治疗方法，其为缺氧诱导因子（HIF）-脯氨酰羟化酶抑制剂。CBC = 全血细胞计数；IM = 肌肉注射；PCV = 红细胞压积（PCV）；PO = 口服；SBP = 收缩压（SBP）。在使用莫利杜司他治疗的CKD人类患者中，该药物可能降低铁调素并增加血细胞比容（HCT）而不降低血清铁，提示HIF-PHI对铁代谢具有有益作用，并且其增加红细胞量的效果在一定程度上不依赖铁补充。然而，由于铁蛋白和铁浓度降低，一些患者仍可能需要铁补充，因此推测部分接受治疗的猫也可能需要铁补充。
Approach to the treatment of anaemia of chronic kidney disease (CKD) with molidustat, a hypoxia-inducible factor–prolyl hydroxylase inhibitor. CBC = complete blood count; IM = intramuscularly; PCV = packed cell volume; PO = orally; SBP = systolic blood pressure In humans with CKD treated with moli-dustat, the drug may lower hepcidin and increase haematocrit without lowering serum iron, 190 suggesting that HIF-PHIs have a beneficial effect on iron metabolism and that their efficacy in increasing red cell mass is less dependent on iron supplementation. However, some patients will require iron supplementation due to reduced ferritin and iron concentrations, 191 so it is presumed there may be a requirement for iron supplementation in some treated cats.

蛋白尿的管理

Management of proteinuria
持续性蛋白尿与猫慢性肾病（CKD）的疾病进展和死亡率相关。62,63,192 持续UPC >0.4的患者（IRIS蛋白尿亚阶段，见表2）应接受肾脏饮食治疗和肾素-血管紧张素-醛固酮系统（RAAS）的药物抑制。RAAS抑制应仅在非脱水且CKD稳定的猫中考虑，以避免可能的不良反应。由于高血压的治疗可以降低蛋白尿，192 高血压猫在收缩压（SBP）<160 mmHg时应重新评估蛋白尿，并在使用额外抗蛋白尿药物之前进行评估。
Persistent proteinuria is associated with disease progression and mortality in cats with CKD.62,63,192 Patients with a persistent UPC of >0.4 (IRIS substage proteinuric, see Table 2) should be treated with a renal diet and pharmacological inhibition of the renin– angiotensin–aldosterone system (RAAS). RAAS inhibition should only be considered in non-dehydrated cats with stable CKD to avoid possible adverse effects. As treatment of hypertension can reduce proteinuria, 192 hypertensive cats should be reassessed for proteinuria once SBP is <160 mmHg and prior to additional antiproteinuric medication.
蛋白尿的治疗选择包括：
Options for the treatment of proteinuria include:
蛋白尿处于边缘水平的猫（UPC 0.2–0.4）相比UPC <0.2的猫更可能发展为肾脏疾病，并具有增加的死亡风险。尽管目前没有证据表明在边缘蛋白尿亚阶段使用抗蛋白尿药物治疗有益，但一些临床医生可能会考虑开具此类药物。RAAS抑制的不良反应可能包括低血压、高钾血症和氮质血症恶化。因此，ACE抑制剂和ARB应谨慎使用，尤其是在晚期CKD猫和脱水猫中。除了监测任何临床恶化外，接受治疗的猫应定期进行复查，如表5所示（结合照护者的资源进行情境化）。肌酐增加>25–30%可能需要审查治疗并调整剂量；任何干预的效果应在约4周后评估。
Cats with borderline proteinuria (UPC 0.2–0.4) may be more likely to develop renal disease and have an increased mortality risk compared with cats with a UPC <0.2.28,62 Although there is no current evidence that treatment with antiproteinuric medication is beneficial at the borderline proteinuric substage, some clinicians may consider prescribing such medication. Adverse effects of RAAS inhibition can include hypotension, hyperkalaemia and worsening azotaemia. Hence, ACE inhibitors and ARBs should be used with care, especially in cats with latestage CKD and in dehydrated cats. As well as monitoring for any clinical deterioration, cats undergoing treatment should be reassessed regularly, as described in Table 5 (contextualising to the caregiver’s resources). An increase in creatinine of >25–30% may warrant review of treatment and a dosage adjustment; the effect of any intervention should be assessed around 4 weeks later.
✜ 替米沙坦，一种血管紧张素受体拮抗剂（ARB）。替米沙坦通过阻止血管紧张素 II 与其受体之一（血管紧张素受体-1）结合来发挥作用。通过这种方式，它可减少慢性肾病（CKD）猫的蛋白尿。替米沙坦用于蛋白尿的剂量为 1 mg/kg 口服，每 24 小时一次。✜ 一种血管紧张素转化酶（ACE）抑制剂，如贝那普利。ACE 抑制剂通过阻断血管紧张素 II 的生成来减少蛋白尿。贝那普利用于蛋白尿的剂量为 0.25–0.5 mg/kg 口服，每 12 小时一次，该 ACE 抑制剂既有片剂形式，也有药物配方食品。表 5. 接受 ARB 或 ACE 抑制剂治疗蛋白尿/边缘蛋白尿猫的监测时间表
✜ Telmisartan, an angiotensin receptor blocker (ARB). Telmisartan prevents angiotensin II from binding to one of its receptors (angiotensin receptor-1). In so doing, it reduces proteinuria in cats with CKD. 193 The dosage of telmisartan for proteinuria is 1 mg/kg PO q24h. ✜ An angiotensin-converting enzyme (ACE) inhibitor, such as benazepril. ACE inhibitors reduce proteinuria by blocking the production of angiotensin II. 194 The dosage of benazepril for proteinuria is 0.25–0.5 mg/kg PO q12h, and this ACE inhibitor is available both in tablet form and as a medicated food. 高血压管理

Management of hypertension
慢性肾脏病（CKD）是高血压的常见潜在原因，所有患有CKD的猫在诊断时以及作为常规监测的一部分都应测量收缩压（SBP）（参见“CKD猫的监测”）。高血压的管理可以防止靶器官损伤，并逆转或改善已有的靶器官损伤临床症状。眼部靶器官损伤通常恢复较慢，但通过高血压治疗可以恢复部分视力，并且高血压性脑病的症状可以逆转。管理的目标是将收缩压（SBP）降低到正常水平（理想情况下<140 mmHg，否则<160 mmHg）。治疗选项列于表6中。应在开始或调整治疗后1–2周重新评估疗效。通常适合门诊管理，尽管对于有严重高血压和靶器官损伤的患者，可能需要住院以便更密切地监测收缩压（SBP）（例如，24小时后）。如果存在显著蛋白尿，可考虑使用血管紧张素II受体拮抗剂（ARB）（例如替米沙坦）作为高血压的初始治疗选择。
CKD is a common underlying cause of hypertension, and all cats with CKD should have their SBP measured at diagnosis and as part of routine monitoring (see ‘Monitoring the cat with CKD’). Management of hypertension can prevent target organ damage and reverse or improve any existing clinical signs of target organ damage. Ocular target organ damage is often slow to resolve but some vision can be regained with treatment of the hypertension, 195 and signs of hypertensive encephalopathy can be reversed. 196 The goal of management is to reduce SBP to a normotensive level (ideally <140 mmHg, but otherwise <160 mmHg).90,197 Options for treatment are listed in Table 6. Efficacy should be reassessed after 1–2 weeks of starting or adjusting therapy. Outpatient management is usually appropriate, although patients with severe hypertension and target organ damage may be hospitalised to allow closer monitoring of SBP (eg, after 24 h). 25 If significant proteinuria is present, an ARB (telmisar-tan) could be considered as an initial choice of therapy for hypertension.
如果尽管猫的血压正常，蛋白尿仍然存在，也可以在使用氨氯地平的基础上使用ARB或ACE抑制剂。在使用药物组合时，应密切监测猫是否出现低血压。表6。
An ARB or ACE inhibitor could also be used in addition to amlodipine if proteinuria persists despite the cat being normotensive. When using combinations of medications, the cat should be monitored closely for hypotension. Table 6.

低血钾的管理

Management of hypokalaemia
低血钾在患有较高阶段慢性肾病（CKD）的猫中很常见，而饮食摄入不足或钾的尿液丢失增加以及RAAS激活被认为是促成因素。钾对正常肌肉功能和胃肠蠕动至关重要，低血钾与嗜睡、厌食、肌病（如颈部下垂和跖行姿势）以及便秘相关。在人类中，钾水平<4.0 mmol/l与终末期肾病和死亡风险增加相关，但低血钾尚未被确定为猫病情进展的风险因素。然而，血清钾水平并不能代表全身组织钾水平，因此血清钾位于低正常范围的猫仍可能存在系统性缺钾。一些临床医生建议即使血清钾在参考范围低端时也应进行补充，目标是维持血清钾 >4 mmol/l。在患有慢性肾病（CKD）和便秘的猫中，维持血清钾浓度尤为重要。
Hypokalaemia is common in cats with higher stages of CKD, and an inadequate dietary intake or increased urinary loss of potassium and activation of the RAAS are thought to be contributing factors. 204 Potassium is vital for normal muscle function and gastrointestinal motility, and hypokalaemia is associated with lethargy, inappetence, myopathy (eg, cervical ventroflexion and plantigrade stance) and constipation. 86 In humans, a potassium level <4.0 mmol/l is associated with an increased risk of end-stage renal disease and mortality, 77 but hypokalaemia has not been identified as a risk factor for progression in cats. However, serum potassium levels are not representative of systemic tissue potassium levels, and so cats with low–normal serum potassium may still be systemically depleted. 205 Some clinicians recommend supplementation even when serum potassium is within the low end of the RI, with a goal of maintaining serum potassium >4 mmol/l. Maintaining serum potassium concentrations is particularly important in cats with CKD and constipation.
肾脏饮食会补充钾，但补充的程度差异很大。在稳定、轻度低钾血症的患者中，转向高钾饮食可能有益。对于中度至重度低钾血症但状况稳定的患者，应补充口服葡萄糖酸钾或柠檬酸钾（每只猫1–4 mEq，每12小时一次，根据反应调整）。对于有酸中毒证据的患者，由于柠檬酸钾的碱化作用更强，可能比葡萄糖酸钾更适合。在代偿失调、严重低钾血症的患者中，需要住院并静脉补充氯化钾，然后在家进行口服补充。
Renal diets are supplemented with potassium, but to a very variable degree. In stable, mildly hypokalaemic patients, switching to a higher potassium diet may be beneficial. In moderately to severely hypokalaemic, but stable, patients, supplementation with oral potassium gluconate or potassium citrate is indicated (1–4 mEq/cat q12h, adjusted according to response). In patients with evidence of acidosis, potassium citrate may be a better choice than potassium gluconate due to its greater alkalinising properties. In decompensated, severely hypokalaemic patients, hospitalisation and intravenous potassium chloride supplementation is necessary, followed by oral supplementation at home.

酸中毒的管理

Management of acidosis
代谢性酸中毒是人体慢性肾病（CKD）进展和死亡率的重要危险因素，206 并且在猫的慢性肾病（CKD）中可能比以前认为的更常见。207 腌尿氨排泄在患有 CKD 的猫中显著受损，并且与血清肌酐浓度呈负相关。207 提供旨在最小化酸中毒的肾脏饮食可能有益；然而，在一些猫中仍可能需要碱性补充，目前正由指南小组成员之一（JQ）进行研究。口服补充柠檬酸钾（40–75 mg/kg 每12小时一次）或碳酸氢钠（10–12 mg/kg 每8–12小时一次）可用于维持血清碳酸氢盐浓度在>16 mmol/l。
Metabolic acidosis is a significant risk factor for CKD progression and mortality in humans, 206 and may be more common in feline CKD than previously thought. 207 Urinary ammonia excretion is significantly impaired in cats with CKD and is inversely associated with serum creatinine concentra-tion. 207 Feeding a renal diet formulated to minimise acidosis may be beneficial; however, alkali supplementation may still be necessary in some cats and is currently under investigation by one of the Guidelines’ panel members (JQ). Oral supplementation with potassium citrate (40–75 mg/kg q12h) or sodium bicarbonate (10–12 mg/kg q8–12h) may be used to maintain serum bicarbonate concentrations >16 mmol/l.

便秘的管理

Management of constipation
与慢性肾病（CKD）相关的便秘的流行率尚未报道；然而，便秘似乎是一个常见的医疗问题，可能引起不适、恶心和食欲减退，从而影响生活质量（图25）。86,149 观察到患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫排便的频率低于健康猫。208,209 与慢性肾病（CKD）相关的便秘的病因可能是水分平衡功能障碍，可能伴有胃肠动力异常。肾脏未能提供适当的尿液浓缩能力，因此慢性亚临床脱水导致水分从结肠被重新吸收以进行补偿。低钾血症、尿毒症毒素、饮食及使用磷结合剂等因素可能还会进一步导致便秘。
The prevalence of constipation associated with CKD has not been reported; however, constipation appears to be a common medical concern and can cause discomfort, nausea and reduced food intake, and thereby affect quality of life (Figure 25).86,149 Cats with CKD are observed to defecate less frequently than healthy cats.208,209 The aetiology of constipation associated with CKD is likely a dysfunction of water balance, possibly combined with abnormal gastrointestinal motility. The kidneys fail to provide appropriate urineconcentrating ability and, hence, chronic sub-clinical dehydration results in water being reabsorbed from the colon to compensate. Factors such as hypokalaemia, uraemic toxins, diet and the use of phosphate binders may additionally contribute to constipation.
图25. 一只患有CKD相关便秘的猫的侧腹部X光片。便秘会减少食物摄入并引起恶心和不适。在对这样的患者进行影像检查时，检查关节可以帮助识别DJD，作为导致排便不情愿和/或不使用猫砂盆的额外促发因素。图片由Lumbry Park提供
Figure 25. Lateral abdominal radiograph of a cat with constipation associated with CKD. Constipation can reduce food intake and cause nausea and discomfort. When imaging such patients, examination of joints can help to identify DJD as an additional contributing factor causing reluctance to defecate and/or use a litter tray. Image courtesy of Lumbry Park

兽医专家

Veterinary Specialists
在评估便秘时，病史非常重要。与照护者讨论的问题包括排便频率、排便时间、粪便稠度、排便难度或用力程度，以及是否伴有呕吐，因为这些信息可能不会主动提供。此外，家中散落的粪便颗粒可能表明排便困难。在每次评估中，应在病历中记录猫的水合状态。体格检查可能发现降结肠内有小而硬的粪便，在盆腔入口前积聚粪便；在更严重的情况下，结肠内可能有大量硬粪堆积。
A clinical history is very important in the assessment of constipation. Questions to discuss with the caregiver include the frequency of defecation, time spent defecating, faecal consistency, the degree of difficulty or straining, and any vomiting associated with defecation, as this information may not be volunteered. Also, faecal pellets left around the house may indicate dyschezia. An assessment of the cat’s hydration status should be noted in the medical record at each assessment. Physical examination may reveal small hard faeces in the descending colon with a build-up of faecal material before the pelvic inlet, or, in more severe cases, a large amount of hard faecal material in the colon.
在患病猫咪中，除了慢性肾病（CKD）之外还需考虑的因素包括：管理疼痛和优化老年猫的猫砂设施可以促进规律排便。由于CKD继发便秘的主要诱因被认为是慢性脱水，因此在采取其他医疗治疗之前，应先评估并改善患者的水合状态。应测量血清钾水平，并在需要时进行补充。在纠正脱水和低钾血症后，可开始口服渗透性通便剂。聚乙二醇（PEG）3350是一种渗透性泻药，已在健康猫中评估，并被发现对软化粪便有效。虽然其在CKD猫中的疗效尚未评价，但它通常被处方使用（每12–24小时1/8至1/4茶匙）；此外，有些人认为它比乳果糖更有效，乳果糖需要肠道微生物处理，可能导致腹胀，而且对许多猫来说难以服用。
Factors additional to the CKD to consider in affected cats include: Managing pain and optimising litter facilities for older cats can encourage regular defecation. As the primary inciting cause of constipation secondary to CKD is considered to be chronic dehydration, the patient’s hydration status should be addressed before other medical therapies are instituted. Serum potassium should be measured and supplemented, if needed. After correction of dehydration and hypokalaemia, oral osmotic stool softeners are introduced. Polyethylene glycol (PEG) 3350 is an osmotic laxative that has been assessed in normal cats and found to be effective as a stool-softening agent. 210 Although its efficacy in cats with CKD has not been evaluated, it is commonly prescribed (one-eighth to one-quarter of a teaspoon q12–24h); moreover, it is thought by some to be more effective than lactulose, which is processed by intestinal microflora and may lead to bloating, as well as being challenging to administer to many cats.
210 另一个重要的考虑因素是 PEG 3350 以粉末形式存在，并且似乎耐受性良好。体积形成剂如车前子（起始剂量为每 24 小时四分之一茶匙，或每 12 小时分次给予）或罐装南瓜（起始剂量为每 24 小时半至一茶匙，或每 12 小时分次给予），在患者水化正常且可混入食物接受的情况下，也可能作为慢性肾病（CKD）相关便秘管理中的纤维来源。211,212 促动力药物是 CKD 相关便秘医疗管理的第二层，因为尚未评估猫 CKD 患者固有结肠蠕动受到影响的程度。西沙必利（Cisapride，口服每 8 小时 1 mg/kg）是最常用的促动力药物，已证明在体外对正常和异常猫结肠平滑肌的收缩性具有积极影响。213,214 ✜ 由退行性关节病（DJD）引起的疼痛，导致不愿排便和/或使用猫砂盆；以及 ✜ 环境因素，包括猫砂盆的可用性/数量，位置和猫砂基质。
210 Another important consideration is that PEG 3350 comes as a powder that appears to be well tolerated. Bulk-forming agents such as psyllium (starting dose one-quarter teaspoon q24h or divided q12h) or canned pumpkin (starting dose one-half to one teaspoon q24h or divided q12h) may also be useful as fibre sources in the management of constipation associated with CKD in patients with normal hydration, if accepted mixed into food.211,212 Promotility agents are a second tier of medical management for constipation associated with CKD, as the degree to which inherent colonic motility is affected has not been assessed in feline CKD patients. Cisapride (1 mg/kg q8h PO) is the most common promotility medication used, and has been demonstrated to have a positive effect on contractility in normal and abnormal feline colonic smooth muscle in vitro.213,214 ✜ Pain from DJD, causing reluctance to defecate and/or use a litter tray; and ✜ Environmental factors, including the availability/number of litter trays, their location and the litter substrate.

尿毒症毒素的管理

Management of uraemic toxins
减少尿毒素生成的策略包括饮食管理（例如避免高蛋白饮食）、使用益生元、益生菌或吸附剂，以及便秘的管理。Renaltec 是一种碳基吸附剂，设计用于结合消化道中的吲哚硫酸前体，最近已被证明可降低患有慢性肾病（CKD）猫的血清尿毒素水平。215,216
Strategies to decrease the production of uraemic toxins include dietary management (eg, avoidance of high protein), use of prebiotics, probiotics or adsorbents, and management of constipation. Renaltec, a carbon-based adsorbent designed to bind indoxyl sulfate precursors in the digestive tract, has recently been demonstrated to decrease serum uraemic toxins in cats with CKD.215,216

未来发展

Future developments
血管内皮功能被认为在CKD猫中受损，而伯拉普洛（Beraprost），一种前列环素类似物，被认为对受损的内皮细胞具有保护作用。伯拉普洛已在一项回顾性队列研究中进行评估。报告显示，患有3期CKD的猫在接受治疗的患者中无进展生存期显著更高。217 图26。在这只猫身上进行采血时，使用可舔的零食作为分散注意力手段，进行温和操作（包括在诊疗台上覆盖柔软材料），并使用内侧隐静脉配合蝶形针进行采血。请注意，以这种方式使用的小量食物/零食不太可能影响血清生化检测结果。图片由 Jessica Quimby 提供。
Vascular endothelial function is thought to be impaired in cats with CKD, and beraprost, a prostacyclin analogue, is thought to have protective effects on injured endothelial cells. Beraprost has been assessed in a retrospective cohort study. Progression-free survival in cats with stage 3 CKD was reported to be significantly higher in treated patients. 217 Figure 26. Blood sampling in this cat is being performed using a lickable treat as a distraction, gentle handling (including a soft cover over the consultation table) and use of the medial saphenous vein with a butterfly catheter. Note that the small amount of food/treats used in this way is unlikely to affect serum biochemistry results. Image courtesy of Jessica Quimby

CKD及重要合并症

CKD and important comorbidities
鉴于老年猫慢性肾病（CKD）的高发率，伴随疾病也很常见。这些伴随疾病不仅可能进一步影响猫的生活质量，还给患者管理带来了诸多挑战。每种疾病的表现症状可能会被并发疾病掩盖。需要服用多种药物会增加护理者的负担，也增加药物相互作用的风险；此外，CKD可能导致治疗并发疾病所用药物的肾脏清除率降低。此外，营养需求可能存在冲突。在为患有多种疾病的猫，尤其是患有CKD和伴随疾病的猫制定最佳营养建议时，可能会面临挑战。第一步是确定每种疾病的营养目标；在某些情况下，需要优先考虑某一目标而非另一目标。
Given the high prevalence of CKD in senior cats, comorbidities are common. Not only can they further impact the cat’s quality of life, but they pose a number of challenges for patient management. Presenting signs of each condition may be masked by the presence of concurrent conditions. The need to give several medications can increase the caregiver burden 225 and also the risk of drug interactions; additionally, CKD can result in reduced renal clearance of medications used to treat concurrent conditions. 226 Moreover, nutritional requirements may conflict. 227 It can be challenging to make optimal nutritional recommendations for cats with multiple diseases, especially those with CKD and comorbid conditions. The first step is to identify nutritional goals for each disease; in some situations, there is a need to prioritise one goal over another.
在某些情况下，可能需要咨询经过认证的兽医营养学专家。表7. 使用“五大支柱”模型作为指南优化慢性肾病（CKD）猫的家庭环境218,220,221
Consultation with a board-certified veterinary nutritionist may be warranted in some cases. Table 7. Optimising the home environment for a cat with CKD using the ‘five pillars’ model as a guide218,220,221
支柱 1：提供安全空间 提供可预测、安全且易于接近的休息场所
Pillar 1: Provide a safe space Provide predictable, secure and readily accessible resting
位于多个地点的区域。它们应该便于有行动障碍或患有退行性关节疾病(DJD)疼痛的猫咪使用，必要时可使用坡道或台阶，并且远离家中繁忙的区域。安静、温暖、阳光充足的休息地点，配有厚实的床垫，可鼓励猫咪使用。
areas in multiple locations. They should be within easy reach for a cat with mobility issues or pain from DJD, with the use of ramps or steps, as necessary, and located away from busy areas of the home. Quiet, warm, sunny resting spots with deep bedding encourage use.

支柱 2：提供多样且分开的资源

Pillar 2: Provide multiple and separated resources
患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫可能需要更频繁地排尿和饮水，因此应方便地使用猫砂盆和多个水源。资源应分布在家中的各个层面，并且数量充足（每只猫至少提供每种资源两份）。食物和水可以放在升高的碗中提供，并为患有退行性关节疾病的猫提供水平抓挠的机会。猫砂盆应低边并使用柔软的垫料，并定期清理。
Cats with CKD may need to urinate and drink more frequently, so should have easy access to litter trays and multiple water sources. Resources should be distributed across all levels of the home and in sufficient number (at least two of each resource per cat). Food and water can be offered in raised bowls, and horizontal scratching opportunities provided for cats with DJD. Litter trays should be low sided with a soft substrate and regularly
支柱 3：提供机会进行捕食行为的游戏和表演 即使是患有慢性疾病的年长猫也会受益
Pillar 3: Provide opportunity to play and perform predatory behaviour Even older cats with chronic illness will benefit from
活动。镇痛可能有助于患有退行性关节病（DJD）的猫咪参与轻度活动和低强度玩耍，这也有助于激发积极的情绪并预防便秘。可以鼓励猫咪在花园里走动或短时间玩钓鱼玩具。结合带有听觉或嗅觉刺激的玩具（例如，沙沙材质、猫薄荷、缬草、银藤）、在可预测的时间和地点玩耍、使用短而一致的活动时间，以及提供侧放的纸板箱，都有助于猫咪安全舒适的参与。
activity. Analgesia may help cats with DJD engage with light activity and low-impact play, which can also help trigger engaging emotions and prevent constipation. Cats could be encouraged to walk around a garden or engage with a fishing toy for short periods. Incorporating toys with auditory or olfactory stimuli (eg, crinkle material, catnip, valerian, silvervine), playing at predictable times and locations, using short, consistent sessions, and offering cardboard boxes placed on their sides supports safe, comfortable engagement.

支柱四：提供积极、一致且可预测的

Pillar 4: Provide positive, consistent and predictable
人–猫社会互动 允许猫选择互动的类型和持续时间，绝不强行抱猫。有些慢性病猫可能会减少梳理自己毛发的次数，但仍然喜欢被刷毛；还有一些猫可能仍然会从照顾者那里偶尔享受一些零食。所有家庭成员都应接受教育，不要随意抱猫，而是应慢慢靠近并使用柔和的声音，以防吓到猫。猫应该被允许在自己选择的时候脱离与人的互动。
human–cat social interaction Allow cats to choose the type and duration of interaction and never force handling. Some cats with chronic illness may groom less and enjoy being brushed; others may still have the appetite for occasional treats from their caregivers. All family members should be educated not to pick up the cat, but approach slowly with a soft voice to prevent startling. The cat should be allowed to disengage from interactions with people at their choosing.
支柱五：提供一个尊重猫感官重要性的环境
Pillar 5: Provide an environment that respects the importance of the cat’s senses

应避免强烈的气味（香水、空气

Strong scents should be avoided (perfumes, air
可以考虑使用空气清新剂和猫费洛蒙产品。可以加热食物以增加香味并鼓励进食。应避免突然的巨大噪音以防惊吓猫咪。图片由Samantha Taylor提供（支柱1、2和5）、国际猫护理组织提供（支柱3）以及Sarah Caney提供（支柱4）。尽管面临挑战，通过关注对猫影响最大的疾病、管理疼痛、支持护理者，同时关注依从性和护理者负担，并监测对治疗的反应，多病共存的老年猫仍然可以享有良好的生活质量。228
fresheners) and use of feline pheromone products considered. Food could be warmed to increase aromas and encourage eating. Sudden loud noises should be avoided to prevent startling the cat. Images courtesy of Samantha Taylor (Pillars 1, 2 and 5), International Cat Care (Pillar 3) and Sarah Caney (Pillar 4) Despite the challenges, by focusing on the disease with the greatest impact on the cat, managing pain, supporting caregivers while remaining mindful of compliance and caregiver burden, and monitoring responses to treatment, senior cats with multiple conditions can enjoy a good quality of life. 228

慢性肾病（CKD）和甲状腺功能亢进

CKD and hyperthyroidism
甲状腺功能亢进是最常见的猫内分泌疾病，主要发生在与慢性肾病（CKD）相同的猫群中。有报告显示，15–51%的甲状腺功能亢进猫同时患有CKD，因此在同一只猫中同时管理这两种疾病并不罕见。这两种疾病的共存为诊断带来一些挑战，原因如下：在没有氮质血症的甲状腺功能亢进猫中很难诊断CKD，因此也难以预测哪些猫在治疗后会出现“显现”的氮质血症。甲状腺功能亢进治疗前SDMA浓度高的猫更可能患有CKD，但SDMA水平正常并不排除猫在恢复甲状腺功能后出现氮质血症的可能性。治疗前尿比重（USG）低于1.035的甲状腺功能亢进猫在治疗后更可能出现氮质血症。一旦患者甲状腺功能恢复正常，重新评估肾功能参数以诊断潜在的CKD是非常重要的。
Hyperthyroidism is the most common feline endocrinopathy and mainly occurs in the same population of cats affected by CKD. Given that 15–51% of cats with hyperthyroidism are reported also to have CKD,229–232 managing both conditions in the same cat is not an unusual scenario. The concurrent presence of the two conditions creates some diagnostic challenges for both illnesses for the following reasons: It is difficult to make a diagnosis of CKD in a cat with hyperthyroidism without azotaemia and, hence, predict which cats will develop ‘unmasked’ azotaemia post-treatment. Cats with high SDMA concentrations prior to treatment for hyperthyroidism are more likely to have CKD, but a normal SDMA level does not exclude the development of azotaemia when the cat becomes euthyroid. 234 Hyperthyroid cats with low USG (<1.035) pretreatment are more likely to develop azotaemia posttreatment. 235 It is important to reassess renal parameters once a patient is euthyroid in order to diagnose the underlying CKD.
管理这些合并症的关键点总结在框9中。患有慢性肾病（CKD）和甲状腺功能亢进的猫可以有相当合理的中位生存期。在诊断甲状腺功能亢进时已经出现氮质血症的猫，其预后比没有氮质血症的猫差，文献记录的生存时间约为6个月到2年。236–238 治疗后出现氮质血症的甲亢猫的生存时间明显短于仍保持非氮质血症的猫（2.8年对4.3年）。232 ✜ 临床症状可能重叠（如体重下降、呕吐、多尿/多饮）； ✜ CKD可能掩盖更明显的甲亢症状，如多食； ✜ 甲状腺功能亢进可能增加肾小球滤过率（GFR）并降低肌肉质量，导致肌酐下降到参考范围内，但在治疗后“显现”； 232 ✜ CKD可能将总T4降至参考范围内； 233 ✜ CKD和甲亢都可能引起继发性高血压；90 因此，在患有这些并发症的猫中，血压测量是优先事项。
Key points in managing these comorbidities are summarised in Box 9. Cats with CKD and hyperthyroidism can have very reasonable median survival times. Cats who are azotaemic at the point of diagnosis of hyperthyroidism have a poorer prognosis than those without azotaemia, with documented survival times of around 6 months to 2 years.236–238 Hyperthyroid cats who become azotaemic after treatment have significantly shorter survival times (2.8 years) than those remaining non-azotaemic (4.3 years). 232 ✜ Clinical signs may overlap (ie, weight loss, vomiting, PU/PD); ✜ CKD may mask more obvious signs of hyperthyroidism, such as polyphagia; ✜ Hyperthyroidism may increase the GFR and decrease muscle mass, with the result that creatinine is lowered into the RI but is ‘unmasked’ post-treatment; 232 ✜ CKD may reduce total thyroxine (T4) into the RI; 233 ✜ Secondary hypertension occurs with both CKD and hyperthyroidism; 90 thus, measurement of blood pressure is a priority in cats with these concurrent conditions.
患有治疗后医源性甲状腺功能减退和氮质血症的猫存活时间明显较短。因此，在患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫中尤其要避免甲状腺功能减退。
Cats with post-treatment iatrogenic hypothyroidism and azotaemia have significantly shorter survival times. 239 Hence, hypothyroidism is particularly important to avoid in cats with CKD.

慢性肾病（CKD）和慢性疼痛

CKD and chronic pain
DJD 是猫慢性疼痛研究最多的原因。然而，许多其他状况也会导致疼痛，例如牙病（图 27）、泌尿道疾病、眼病和肿瘤；持续的术后疼痛、神经性疼痛和医学相关的疼痛（来自胃肠/肝脏疾病）也是慢性疼痛的其他形式。虽然慢性肾病（CKD）本身不太可能引起疼痛，但相关状况如脱水、恶心、肾盂肾炎、泌尿结石以及高血压引起的靶器官疾病可能会引起不适。图 27. 一只患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫的吸收性病变。在慢性肾病（CKD）患者中应管理牙病以减少疼痛。反过来，这可以改善食物摄入和长期健康。图片由
DJD is the most studied cause of chronic pain in cats. 240 However, many other conditions will result in pain, such as dental disease (Figure 27), 241 urinary tract disease, ocular disease and neoplasia; persistent postoperative pain, neuropathic pain and medical pain (from gastrointestinal/hepatic disease) are other forms of chronic pain.242,243 While CKD itself is unlikely to be painful, associated conditions such as dehydration, nausea, pyelonephritis, urolithiasis and target organ disease from hypertension could cause discomfort. Figure 27. Resorptive lesion in a cat with CKD. Dental disease should be managed in CKD patients to reduce pain. In turn, this can improve food intake and long-term health. Image courtesy of

萨曼莎·泰勒

Samantha Taylor
慢性疼痛应使用多模式方法进行管理，理想情况下应包括药物疗法和非药物疗法（例如环境调整）。对于慢性肾病（CKD）患者而言，对镇痛药副作用的担忧可能导致疼痛得不到有效管理，因此识别和治疗疼痛应被视为CKD猫管理方法的一部分。同样，可以减轻疼痛的操作，如拔牙，也不应因存在CKD而避免，尽管在开药或计划麻醉时当然必须仔细考虑肾脏情况。
Chronic pain should be managed using a multimodal approach, ideally involving pharmacological and non-pharmacological therapies (eg, environmental modifications). 244 For the CKD patient specifically, the fear of adverse effects of analgesics could result in unmanaged pain, and so it is important that the identification and treatment of pain is considered part of the management approach for cats with CKD. Similarly, procedures that could reduce pain, such as dental extractions, should not be avoided because of the presence of CKD, although the kidneys must, of course, be carefully considered when prescribing medications or planning anaesthesia.245,246

在患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫中使用非甾体抗炎药（NSAID）

Use of non-steroidal anti-inflammatory drugs in cats with CKD
《2024 ISFM 和 AAFP 关于猫长期使用非甾体抗炎药（NSAID）（NSAIDs）的共识指南》中提供了全面的指导，支持兽医在慢性疼痛情况下（包括慢性肾病（CKD）猫）使用非甾体抗炎药（NSAID）处方的决策。在当前指南中，需要注意的是，目前的证据有限，既无法充分支持也无法否定慢性疼痛伴慢性肾病（CKD）的猫长期使用NSAIDs的安全性。早期的大部分数据都是回顾性研究，关于美洛昔康和罗贝昔昔布的研究受到选择性偏差、稳定CKD定义差异以及NSAID给药时间相对较短的影响。然而，现有证据表明，对于稳定的CKD猫，低剂量的美洛昔康或罗贝昔昔布在特定条件下谨慎使用是可行的，因为研究未显示这些患者出现可检测的肾损伤。如果要使用NSAIDs，患者的选择至关重要。
Comprehensive guidance to support veterinarians in decision-making around prescribing non- steroidal anti-inflammatory drugs (NSAIDs) in situations of chronic pain, including cats with CKD, is provided in the ‘2024 ISFM and AAFP consensus guidelines on the long-term use of NSAIDs in cats’. 247 For the purpose of these current Guidelines, it is important to note that there is limited evidence either supporting or refuting the longterm use of NSAIDs in cats with chronic pain and concomitant CKD. Much of the earlier data was retrospective in nature, with studies of meloxicam and robenacoxib hampered by selective bias, differing definitions of stable CKD and relatively short periods of administration of the NSAID. However, current evidence suggests that low doses of meloxicam or robenacoxib can be administered cautiously to cats with stable CKD, as studies have not demonstrated detectable renal injury in these patients under specific conditions.248–251 Patient selection is vital if NSAIDs are to be used.
在患有不稳定（进展性）慢性肾病（CKD）的猫、脱水、血容量不足、低血压或身体状况不佳的猫，或者患有IRIS 4期CKD的猫中，应避免使用这些药物。在使用NSAID时，如果猫的CKD处于稳定状态，定义为在至少2个月的时间里体重和肌酐浓度变化很小，并且对同时存在的疾病（如高血压、高磷血症和尿路感染）进行了控制。如果要给患有IRIS 1期或2期CKD的猫使用NSAID，它们应该保持水合状态，正常进食和饮水，并告知饲主潜在的不良反应并获得知情同意。对于某些3期猫的治疗也可能考虑使用，但在这一组猫中NSAID的使用研究较少。
These drugs should be avoided in cats with unstable (progressive) CKD, cats who are dehydrated, hypovolaemic, hypotensive or otherwise unwell, or those with IRIS stage 4 CKD. Stable CKD in cats, in the context of prescribing NSAIDs, is defined as minimal changes in body weight and creatinine concentration over a period of at least 2 months, with control of concurrent conditions such as hypertension, hyperphosphataemia and UTI. If NSAIDs are to be prescribed to cats with IRIS stage 1 or 2 CKD, they should be hydrated and eating and drinking normally, with caregivers informed of potential adverse effects and providing informed consent. Treatment of some stage 3 cats may also be considered, but use of NSAIDs is less studied in this group.
猫咪在使用非甾体抗炎药（NSAID）进行疼痛管理时，应监测其疗效和不良反应，并对护理人员进行教育，确保猫咪的饮水量得到维持。建议进行常规检查，以便为个体猫咪逐步减少到最低有效剂量，并及早发现不良反应。NSAID 与 ACE 抑制剂、ARB 或氨氯地平联合使用可能增加低血压和不良反应的风险。此外，NSAID 可能降低氨氯地平/ARB 的降压效果（在人体中已有文献记录），因此应避免联合用药，或如必须使用，应密切监测猫咪。
Cats should be monitored for both efficacy of the NSAID for pain management and for adverse effects, and caregivers educated on maintaining the cat’s water intake. Routine checks are recommended to allow tapering to the lowest effective dose for the individual cat and for early recognition of adverse effects. 247 The combination of NSAID and ACE inhibitor, ARB or amlodipine could increase the risk of hypotension and adverse effects. Additionally, NSAIDs could reduce the antihypertensive effects of amlodipine/ARBs (as documented in humans 252 ), and so combination therapy should be avoided, or, if used, the cat should be closely monitored.

慢性肾病（CKD）猫的替代镇痛药

Alternative analgesics for chronic pain in cats with CKD
当NSAIDs被认为不适合使用，或需要额外的镇痛时，可能需要选择替代药物。可能的药物及其使用的证据总结如下。重要的是，这些药物大多数在疗效方面研究不足，因此应监测其有效性，最好使用客观的疼痛评分，例如猫肌肉骨骼疼痛指数（Feline Musculoskeletal Pain Index）。监测，包括慢性疼痛评估，对于正在使用镇痛药的CKD患者至关重要。由于这些猫排泄功能受损，可能发生药物积累、过量和不良反应。相反，剂量不足可能导致低于治疗浓度，从而引起持续疼痛或产生误导性的安慰剂效应。
When NSAIDs are not considered suitable, or additional analgesia is needed, alternative medication choices may be required. Possible drugs, and the evidence supporting their use, are summarised below. Importantly, most of these drugs are understudied in terms of efficacy, and so effectiveness should be monitored, ideally with an objective pain scale such as the Feline Musculoskeletal Pain Index. 253 Monitoring, including chronic pain assessment, is crucial in CKD patients on analgesics. Drug accumulation, overdosing and adverse effects may occur as excretion is impaired in these cats. Conversely, underdosing may result in subtherapeutic concentrations, leading to continuous pain or misleading placebo effects.

CKD和慢性肠病

CKD and chronic enteropathy
✜ 加巴喷丁类药物 在一项研究中，254 研究发现加巴喷丁会降低老年猫的活动水平，这可能是由于其镇静作用，同时与安慰剂相比还能改善受损的活动。然而，患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫血清中加巴喷丁水平较高，因此可能需要调整剂量以避免不良反应。223 尽管研究较少，对普瑞巴林也存在类似的担忧。255 对患有CKD的猫使用加巴喷丁类药物的剂量方案尚未确定。 ✜ Frunevetmab 抗神经生长因子单克隆抗体frunevetmab用于管理猫的退行性关节病（DJD），256 但目前尚无关于在同时患有DJD和CKD的猫中该药物安全性的已发布数据。
✜ Gabapentinoids In one study, 254 gabapentin decreased activity in geriatric cats, potentially due to sedative effects, while also improving impaired activities compared with placebo. However, cats with CKD have higher serum levels of gabapentin, so dosage adjustment may be required to avoid adverse effects. 223 Although less well studied, similar concerns apply to pregabalin. 255 Dosage regimens for gabapentinoids in cats with CKD have not been established. ✜ Frunevetmab The anti-nerve growth factor monoclonal antibody frunevetmab is used to manage DJD in cats, 256 but there is currently no published data on the safety of the drug in cats with concurrent DJD and CKD.
✜ 曲马多 合成阿片类镇痛药曲马多已被证明可以改善患有骨关节炎的猫的活动能力，257 但尚未在患有慢性肾病 (CKD) 的猫中进行研究。 ✜ 金刚烷胺 一项盲法、安慰剂对照研究表明，金刚烷胺（一种N-甲基-D-天冬氨酸受体拮抗剂）可以改善由主人识别的骨关节炎猫的活动受限和生活质量；258 然而，治疗组的活动量有所下降，可能是由于镇静作用。该药物尚未在患有CKD的猫中进行研究。 ✜ 布比卡因 口腔黏膜透过性布比卡因可以有效镇痛，但长期使用不太实用。259 目前尚无研究调查阿片类药物在猫慢性疼痛中的使用。
✜ Tramadol The synthetic opioid analgesic tramadol has been demonstrated to improve mobility in cats with osteoarthritis, 257 but has not been studied in cats with CKD. ✜ Amantadine A blinded, placebo-controlled study showed that amantadine, an antagonist of N-methyl-D-aspartate receptors, improved owner-identified impaired mobility and quality of life in cats with osteoarthritis; 258 however, activity was decreased, potentially due to sedation, in the treatment group. This drug has not been studied in cats with CKD. ✜ Buprenorphine Oral transmucosal buprenorphine can be effective for analgesia, but is less practical for long-term use. 259 There are no studies investigating the use of opioids for feline chronic pain.
✜ 皮下给药氯胺酮 皮下途径使用氯胺酮用于慢性疼痛的剂量据传为每月 0.5 mg/kg。尚需进一步研究，但初步研究表明，当与其他镇痛药联合使用时，对患有骨关节炎的犬只的疼痛可能有益。260 如前所述，慢性肾病（CKD）最关心的营养素是磷。慢性肠病（如食物反应性肠病、慢性炎症性肠病）的营养管理通常围绕限制膳食抗原暴露（如单一蛋白-单一碳水化合物饮食 vs 水解饮食）进行。261 因此，选择此类饮食，但确保磷含量适当，是可能的；或者，也可以向食物中添加磷结合剂。
✜ Subcutaneous ketamine Ketamine administration via the subcutaneous route is anecdotally used for chronic pain at a dosage of 0.5 mg/kg monthly. Further study is required, but initial work suggested a beneficial effect on pain in dogs with osteoarthritis when combined with other analgesics. 260 As mentioned, the primary nutrient of concern for CKD is phosphorus. Nutritional management of chronic enteropathy (eg, food-responsive enteropathy, chronic inflammatory enteropathy) typically revolves around limiting dietary antigen exposure (eg, single protein + single carbohydrate diet vs a hydrolysed diet). 261 Hence, selecting such a diet, but with an appropriate phosphate content, may be possible; alternatively, phosphate binders could be added to the food.
一些制造商生产的水解饮食也是限制磷的。随访评估血清磷可以根据患者反应进行监测和调整。管理慢性肠病可能需要其他措施，例如补充纤维212或免疫调节。
Some manufacturers produce hydrolysed diets that are also phosphate restricted. Follow-up assessment of serum phosphate would allow monitoring and adjustment according to patient response. Other measures to manage chronic enteropathy may be required, such as fibre supplementation 212 or immunomodulation.

慢性肾脏疾病（CKD）和糖尿病

CKD and diabetes mellitus
为了管理患有糖尿病的猫，可以引入低碳水化合物饮食。对于同时患有慢性肾病（CKD）和糖尿病的猫，制定具体的营养建议是一种平衡行为，需要在提供低磷饮食与避免过高碳水化合物负荷之间取得平衡。一些罐装的兽医治疗肾脏饮食的碳水化合物含量足够低（<5 克/100 千卡），可以喂给患有这些合并症的猫。如果无法完全喂食罐装饮食，应优先提供碳水化合物较高、磷较低的饮食（相比喂食碳水化合物较低、磷较高的饮食），并且仍有可能实现糖尿病缓解。
To manage a cat with diabetes mellitus, a low carbohydrate diet may be introduced.262,263 Making specific nutritional recommendations for a cat with CKD and diabetes is a balancing act between feeding a reduced phosphate diet and trying to avoid an excessively high carbohydrate load. Some canned veterinary therapeutic renal diets are sufficiently low in carbohydrates (<5 g/100 kcal) to be fed to a cat with these comorbid conditions. If it is not feasible to feed a canned diet exclusively, the priority should be feeding a higher carbohydrate, lower phosphate diet (vs feeding a lower carbohydrate, higher phosphate diet), and diabetic remission may still be achieved.
重要的是要记住，虽然干粮饮食通常比罐装饮食提供更多的碳水化合物，但并非所有罐装饮食的碳水化合物都很低。许多糖尿病猫咪存在肥胖现象，而肥胖可能导致胰岛素抵抗。如果猫咪肥胖（即体况评分 BCS 8–9/9），则应额外考虑喂食热量密度较低、蛋白质浓度较高的饮食，以帮助在猫咪减肥过程中维持肌肉质量。在这种情况下，减重目标可能是达到 BCS 6–7/9，以获得长期生存益处，同时监测磷水平，并在需要时添加磷结合剂。
It is important to remember that while dry diets generally provide more carbohydrates than canned diets, not all canned diets are low in carbohydrates. Many cats with diabetes mellitus are obese, and obesity can contribute to insulin resistance. If the cat is obese (ie, BCS 8–9/9), then additional consideration should be given to feeding a diet with a lower calorie density and higher protein concentration to aid in the maintenance of muscle mass as the cat loses weight. In this case, the goal for weight loss may be to achieve a BCS of 6–7/9 for long-term survival benefits, with monitoring of phosphate and the addition of a phosphate binder, if required.

糖尿病性肾病

Diabetic nephropathy
最近有证据表明，猫可能会发展早期糖尿病性肾病。然而，它们在其生命周期中可能不会经历足以导致明显糖尿病性肾病的长期血糖暴露，这一点在人类医学中有所观察。报道的糖尿病猫微量白蛋白尿（70%）和蛋白尿（39%至75%）的患病率，也可能提示存在糖尿病性肾病特有的肾小球病理。由于糖尿病猫通常不会出现肾小球滤过率（GFR）下降的明显糖尿病性肾病，因此猫血清肌酐会逐渐升高的情况不太可能发生。
There is recent evidence that cats may develop early diabetic nephropathy. 264 However, they likely do not experience a cumulative glucose exposure in their life span sufficient to cause overt diabetic nephropathy, as is observed in human medicine. The reported prevalence of microalbuminuria (70%) 265 and proteinuria (39% 266 to 75% 265 ) in diabetic cats may also suggest the presence of the glomerular pathology that characterises diabetic nephropathy. As progression to overt diabetic nephropathy, in which there is declining GFR, is not typically recognised in diabetic cats, it is unlikely a cat will have a progressive increase in serum creatinine.
然而，应监测尿蛋白肌酐比（UPC）值（UPC）。如果患有糖尿病的猫在没有活动性尿沉渣的情况下持续出现蛋白尿，建议进行RAAS抑制。如果新诊断的糖尿病猫出现蛋白尿，可以考虑使用钠–葡萄糖共转运蛋白2抑制剂（SGLT2i）而非胰岛素治疗，因为这类药物还能提供额外的肾脏保护作用。关于SGLT2i治疗的更多信息，可参见《2025年iCatCare猫糖尿病诊断与管理共识指南》。
However, the UPC should be monitored. RAAS inhibition is recommended if a cat with diabetes mellitus becomes persistently proteinuric in the absence of an active urinary sediment. Consideration could be given to the use of a sodium–glucose cotransporter-2 inhibitor (SGLT2i) over insulin therapy in a newly diagnosed diabetic cat who is proteinuric, because of the additional renoprotective benefits this class of drug offers. Further information on SGLT2i therapy is provided in the ‘2025 iCatCare consensus guidelines on the diagnosis and management of diabetes melli-tus in cats’. 267

监测患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫

Monitoring the cat with CKD
对患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫进行监测检查的目的是了解患者的进展（改善或恶化）、遵医行为的挑战以及其他护理者支持需求，并且应根据猫和护理者的因素（如性格和经济状况）进行个体化。对于患有糖尿病的猫，已有描述一系列的兽医护理措施，类似的原则也可应用于患有CKD的猫；换句话说，应根据护理者的能力个体化治疗目标和监测，以优化生活质量，同时考虑猫的心理健康，而不影响护理。例如，对在检查过程中感到压力的猫进行重复预约可能会造成伤害；因此，监测可能包括护理者的口头或电子邮件报告。
Monitoring checks for cats with CKD are aimed at understanding patient progress (improvements and deteriorations), compliance challenges and other caregiver support needs, and should be individualised in the light of cat and caregiver factors such as temperament and financial A spectrum of veterinary care is described for cats with diabetes mellitus, 272 and similar principles can be applied to cats with CKD; in other words, individualising treatment goals and monitoring to optimise quality of life within the capacity of the caregiver, and taking into account the mental wellbeing of the cat, without compromising care. For example, repeated appointments for a cat who is distressed by the examination process could cause harm; monitoring might, therefore, incorporate verbal/email reports from the caregiver.
更广泛地说，护士/技术人员的参与在保持联系、进行检查咨询以及支持护理人员遵从饮食建议和施用治疗药物方面可能是非常宝贵的（见补充材料中的“慢性肾病（CKD）猫咪的护理”）。在初次诊断CKD或猫咪临床状况发生任何重大变化后，相对频繁地（重新）评估猫咪的进展可能会有帮助——例如，每2–4周一次，具体取决于患者的IRIS分期和临床症状的严重程度。这将有助于识别需要更多干预的进展性CKD猫咪。如前所述，如适用，监测不一定总是需要检查猫咪，也可以通过评估护理人员的记录以及通过电子邮件或口头收到的报告来进行。在病情稳定的CKD猫咪中，检查的频率可以减少到每3–6个月一次，包括详细的病史采集和体格检查（见框10）。
More generally, nurse/technician involvement can be invaluable in maintaining contact, performing check-up consultations and supporting caregiver compliance with instituting dietary recommendations and administering therapeutic medications (see ‘Nursing the cat with chronic kidney disease’ in the supplementary material). Following the initial diagnosis of CKD or any significant changes in the cat’s clinical condition, it can be helpful to (re)assess the cat’s progress relatively frequently – for example, every 2–4 weeks, depending on the patient’s IRIS stage and the severity of their clinical signs. This will allow identification of cats with more progressive CKD who may require more intervention. 72 As noted, and if appropriate, monitoring need not necessarily always entail examining the cat, but can involve evaluation of caregiver records and reports received via email or verbally. In cats with stable CKD, the frequency of check-ups can be reduced to every 3–6 months, incorporating a detailed history and physical examination (Box 10).
通过适当的教育和培训，照护者通常可以成为临床观察的重要贡献者；例如，通过提供有关其猫的食欲、排便行为和水合状态的信息。鼓励使用结团猫砂可以更精确地监测排尿模式，而监测排便频率和粪便稠度可以帮助识别水合不足和低钾血症，因为脱水或低钾血症的猫往往排便次数较少且粪便干硬。排便频率的减少是脱水的重要潜在指标。在病情稳定的慢性肾病（CKD）猫中，如果表现良好，3至6个月一次的实验室检查（类似于初次评估患者时）可用于评估疾病阶段及可通过干预获益的并发症。此外，临床医生需保持警惕，防止任何新的伴随疾病的发生。
With appropriate education and training, caregivers can often become valuable contributors to clinical insight; for example, by providing information on their cat’s appetite, toileting behaviour and hydration status. Encouraging the use of clumping cat litter can allow more precise monitoring of urination patterns, while monitoring defecation frequency and faecal consistency can help identify poor hydration and hypokalaemia, as dehydrated or hypokalaemic cats tend to defecate less often and pass hard, dry faeces. A reduction in defecation frequency is an important potential indicator of dehydration.208,209 In cats with stable CKD who are doing well, laboratory tests at the 3- to 6-monthly consultations may (similar to the initial assessment of patients) be aimed at assessing the stage of disease and the presence of complications that would benefit from intervention. In addition, the clinician needs to remain vigilant to the development of any new concurrent conditions.
如前所述，大多数慢性肾病（CKD）患者是年纪较大的猫，同时存在的疾病包括甲状腺功能亢进、认知功能障碍、牙周病和退行性关节病（DJD），这些情况很常见。81,227,228 因此，定期实验室评估理想上应包括血压测量、尿液分析、血液学、血清生化（肌酐、磷、、电解质[包括钙和镁，如可能]及白蛋白）以及总T4测定。在血磷处于IRIS目标范围内的猫中，如果可行，测定FGF23浓度可能有助于指导磷限制。在肌肉量不足的猫中，SDMA评估可能很有帮助，因为肌酐测量可能会错误地显示较低的IRIS阶段。
As mentioned, most CKD patients are older cats, and concurrent diseases including hyperthyroidism, cognitive dysfunction, periodontal disease and DJD are com-mon.81,227,228 Therefore, periodic laboratory assessment should ideally include blood pressure measurement, urinalysis, haematology, serum biochemistry (creatinine, phosphate, electrolytes [including calcium and magnesium, where possible] and albumin) and measurement of total T4. In cats with phosphate within the IRIS target range, the inclusion of FGF23 concentrations, where feasible, may be helpful in guiding phosphate restriction. SDMA assessment can be helpful in poorly muscled cats, as creatinine measurement may falsely indicate a lower IRIS stage.
采取分层护理的方法进行实验室检测，考虑到护理者和猫的因素，可能意味着只有在猫心动过速或有甲状腺肿时才测量 T4，将血液学检测限制在血细胞比容（HCT）（PCV），关注生化分析的关键元素（例如，仅进行“肾功能面板”），并在适当时要求护理者在家中收集尿样。对于正在接受肾蛋白尿治疗的猫，应考虑常规尿液分析，以重新评估尿蛋白/肌酐比（UPC）并确定抗蛋白尿治疗的剂量调整。然而，大多数慢性肾病（CKD）猫为非蛋白尿（见表2），因此常规尿液分析可能不必要。如果进行尿液分析并获得活动沉渣或尿培养阳性，应注意记录下泌尿道下部症状或提示肾盂肾炎的症状的存在与否（见框6）。
A spectrum-of-care approach to laboratory testing, allowing for caregiver and cat factors, may mean measuring T4 only if the cat is tachycardic or has a goitre, limiting haematology to a PCV, focusing on the key elements of a biochemistry profile (eg, running just a ‘renal panel’) and asking caregivers to collect a urine sample at home, if indicated. Routine urinalysis should be considered for cats being treating for renal proteinuria in order to reevaluate the UPC and determine dose adjustments for antiproteinuric therapy. However, most cats with CKD are non-proteinuric (see Table 2) and, hence, routine urinalysis is unlikely to be necessary. If urinalysis is performed and an active sediment or positive urine culture is obtained, care should be taken to document the presence or absence of lower urinary tract signs or signs suggestive of pyelonephritis (see Box 6).
对于已经发现并发症的患者，未来复查的时间将取决于具体的并发症以及所采取的干预措施（例如，在开始或更改降压治疗后 5–7 天可能会重新评估收缩压（SBP））。在经济条件有限的情况下，尤其是在慢性肾病（CKD）晚期（晚期 IRIS 3 期和 4 期），即使缺乏详细的实验室数据，控制临床症状如食欲不振、恶心或水合状态不良也可能非常有效。在这些情况下，护士/技师会诊值得考虑。
In patients where complications have been identified, the timing of future check-ups will depend on the complication and intervention applied (eg, SBP might be reassessed 5–7 days after starting or changing antihypertensive therapy). Where finances are constrained, and especially in the later stages of CKD (late IRIS stage 3 and stage 4), control of clinical signs such as poor appetite, nausea or poor hydration status can be highly effective even in the absence of detailed laboratory data. Nurse/technician consultations are worthy of consideration in these cases.

慢性肾病（CKD）猫的预后

Prognosis for cats with CKD
患有慢性肾病（CKD）的猫的预后高度可变且不可预测。许多被诊断患有该疾病并按照本指南概述的适当支持性护理进行管理的猫，可以多年保持稳定和健康。然而，疾病进展（与稳定相比）对生存时间有显著影响，进展性CKD猫的中位生存时间为287天（95%置信区间[CI] 246–354），而稳定性CKD猫为894天（CI 812–1052）。尽可能详细地跟踪患者有助于识别那些疾病进展较快的猫，从而能够在这种情况下为照顾者提供建议和支持。
The prognosis for cats with CKD is highly variable and unpredictable. Many cats diagnosed with the disease and managed with appropriate supportive care, as outlined in these Guidelines, remain stable and well for years. However, progression (vs stability) has a significant influence on survival time, with cats with progressive CKD having a median survival time of 287 days (95% confidence interval [CI] 246–354) vs 894 days (CI 812–1052) for cats with stable CKD. 72 Tracking patients in as much detail as possible assists in identifying those cats whose disease is progressing at a faster rate and, in turn, advising and supporting caregivers in this situation.
对于晚期慢性肾脏病（CKD）猫咪的姑息护理选项在第11框中有所概述。CKD在老年猫中很常见，最常见的原因是病因不明的肾小管间质肾炎。它会导致肾小球滤过率（GFR）下降，从而引起血清磷升高、CKD相关骨矿物质代谢紊乱（CKD-MBD）和尿毒素积累，以及贫血、高钙血症、高血压和蛋白尿等并发症。猫咪可以得到良好的管理，保持较高的生活质量并延长生存时间。当护理人员在兽医团队的支持下，让猫咪转入肾脏专用饮食、服用药物并促进水分摄入时，这些效果会得到更好实现。
Palliative care options for cats with latestage CKD are outlined in Box 11. CKD is common in senior cats and most frequently caused by tubulointerstitial nephritis of unknown aetiology. It results in a reduction in GFR and, hence, consequences such as increased serum phosphate, CKD-MBD and the accumulation of uraemic toxins, as well as complications such as anaemia, hypercal-caemia, hypertension and proteinuria. Cats can be well managed, with a good quality of life and prolonged survival times. These outcomes are facilitated when caregivers are supported by the veterinary team to transition their cats onto renal diets, administer medications and encourage hydration.
摘要要点 ✜ 慢性肾病（CKD）的临床表现包括体重下降、多饮多尿、呕吐、嗜睡和食欲减退，以及便秘。 ✜ 在疾病的早期阶段，症状可能不易被注意到。 ✜ 诊断基于血清肌酐和/或SDMA测定，以及尿液分析和肾脏影像学检查。
Summary Points ✜ cElClinical signs of CKD include weight loss, PU/PD, vomiting, lethargy and hyporexia, along with constipation. ✜ Signs may go unnoticed in the early stages of disease. ✜ ElDiagnosis is based on serum creatinine and/or SDMA measurement, plus urinalysis and imaging of the kidneys.

sj-docx-1-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553 – 2026 iCatCare 补充材料 sj-docx-1-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553.docx (90.5KB, docx) 慢性肾病（CKD）猫咪护理者监测表格。 sj-pdf-1-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553 – 2026 iCatCare 补充材料 sj-pdf-1-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553.pdf (1.3MB, pdf) 慢性肾病（CKD）猫咪管理：护理者指南。 ✜ 诊断后，2-4 周后重复测量肌酐浓度可以区分进展性 M 与稳定性 CKD，这具有预后意义。 ✜ 与 CKD 进展相关的因素包括蛋白尿严重程度增加、血清磷浓度升高、尿毒素堆积和贫血。
sj-docx-1-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553 – Supplemental material for 2026 iCatCare sj-docx-1-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553.docx (90.5KB, docx) Monitoring form for caregivers of cats with chronic kidney disease. sj-pdf-1-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553 – Supplemental material for 2026 iCatCare sj-pdf-1-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553.pdf (1.3MB, pdf) Managing the cat with chronic kidney disease: a guide for caregivers. ✜ Following diagnosis, repeat measurement of creatinine concentration 2-4 weeks later can differentiate progressive M from stable CKD, which is of prognostic significance. ✜ Factors associated with progression of CKD include increasing severity of proteinuria, raised serum phosphate M concentrations, accumulation of uraemic toxins and anaemia.
✜ 一旦确诊慢性肾病（CKD），应根据IRIS分期方案进行分期及亚分期。M 管理重点是通过控制血清磷升高、治疗高血压M ✜ 和蛋白尿（如存在），以及管理其他异常如贫血、低钾血症、恶心/呕吐/食欲减退和便秘来控制CKD-MBD。✜ 由于CKD，不应停止麻醉或镇痛治疗，但应规划手术和M ✜ 治疗方案，以确保不会进一步影响肾功能。sj-pdf-2-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553 – 2026 iCatCare 补充材料 sj-pdf-2-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553.pdf (4.5MB, pdf) 慢性肾病（CKD）猫的护理。
✜ Staging, followed by substaging, should be performed once a diagnosis of CKD is made, based on the IRIS staging scheme. M Management is focused on the control of CKD-MBD by addressing raised serum phosphate, treating hypertension M ✜ and proteinuria, if present, and managing other abnormalities such as anaemia, hypokalaemia, nausea/vomiting/ hyporexia and constipation. ✜ Neither anaesthesia nor analgesic therapy should be withheld due to CKD, but procedures and M ✜ treatment regimens should be planned to ensure no further renal impacts. sj-pdf-2-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553 – Supplemental material for 2026 iCatCare sj-pdf-2-jfm-10.1177_1098612X261466553.pdf (4.5MB, pdf) Nursing the cat with chronic kidney disease.
